# UI v2 — Mobile Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Dựng lại toàn bộ giao diện app Expo theo mock v2 đã chốt (34 màn, 6 luồng A–F) và nối với API backend mới (plan `2026-10-11-ui-v2-backend.md`). Nút đăng nhập Apple/Google/Facebook có trên UI nhưng chỉ báo "Sắp ra mắt".

**Architecture:** Giữ Expo Router + TanStack Query + cấu trúc `src/features/*`. Thay bộ UI primitives trong `src/ui` bằng bộ token/thành phần v2 (màu, font Be Vietnam Pro, Button, Chip, Toggle, Sheet, DatePickerSheet, TabBar…). Điều hướng mới: nhóm `(auth)` và `(app)` với 5 tab `index · journal · create · partner · me`; popup là component `Sheet` (Modal) trong màn hình, không phải route. Logic thuần (lịch, gom nhật ký, lặp lại, hiệu ứng ảnh, nét vẽ) tách file riêng có unit test; màn hình có test RNTL với `mockApi`.

**Tech Stack:** Expo SDK 57 (React Native 0.86, React 19.2, TypeScript 6), expo-router (`expo-router/js-tabs`), TanStack Query v5, @shopify/react-native-skia 2.6.2, expo-camera, expo-image-picker, expo-image, expo-location, expo-linear-gradient (mới), @expo-google-fonts/be-vietnam-pro (mới), Jest (jest-expo) + @testing-library/react-native 14.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-10-couple-app-design.md` (§12 UI v2). **Thiết kế:** `docs/design/mock-v2/*.dc.html` — nguồn chuẩn về màu, cỡ chữ, khoảng cách, bo góc và **nội dung chữ** của từng màn; bảng ánh xạ file ↔ route ở `docs/design/mock-v2/README.md`.

## Global Constraints

- Chỉ thêm 3 dependency: `expo-linear-gradient`, `@expo-google-fonts/be-vietnam-pro` (Task 1) và `expo-clipboard` (Task 6), cài bằng `npx expo install`. Không thêm thư viện UI/icon khác; icon dùng `@expo/vector-icons` (Feather + Ionicons) qua `src/ui/Icon.tsx`.
- Token màu (`src/ui/theme.ts`): nền `#FFF8F3`, bề mặt `#FFFFFF`, nền phụ `#F6EEEA`, viền `#EADFD9`, chữ `#2B1A2E`, chữ phụ `#6F5B6A`, hồng `#E9557A`, hồng đậm `#C9365C`, hồng nhạt `#FFE6EC`, cam nhạt `#FFEEDF`/`#B5562A`, tím nhạt `#EEEAFF`/`#5B4BC4`, xanh nhạt `#E3F5EC`/`#24795A`, đỏ chu kỳ `#D64545`. Chữ trắng chỉ đặt trên `#C9365C` hoặc đậm hơn.
- Font: Be Vietnam Pro 400/500/600/700/800. Mọi chữ hiển thị qua `AppText`.
- Copy tiếng Việt lấy đúng từ mock. Không dùng emoji trong UI (chỉ icon).
- Màn chính (5 tab) luôn có tab bar; màn con có nút quay lại/đóng. Mọi chọn ngày/giờ dùng `DatePickerSheet`.
- Vùng chạm ≥ 44px; nút icon có `accessibilityLabel`; toggle dùng `accessibilityRole="switch"`.
- Test: `cd mobile && npx jest <đường dẫn>`; kiểm kiểu: `cd mobile && npx tsc --noEmit`. Mỗi task kết thúc bằng commit `feat(mobile): …` có dòng `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- Plan backend phải xong trước Task 5 (các hook gọi endpoint mới); Task 1–4 làm được trước.

## Review Focus

1. Người dùng chưa ghép đôi mở mọi tab (Trang chủ, Nhật ký, +, Người ấy, Tôi) → không màn nào lỗi vì `couple === null`; Người ấy hiện thẻ kết nối. Test ở Task 6 và Task 13.
2. Kỷ niệm không có ảnh (khoảnh khắc chỉ chữ) xuất hiện ở Nhật ký lưới, tháng và xem theo ngày → hiện thẻ chữ, không crash vì `media[0]` undefined. Test ở Task 12.
3. Chọn ngày ở popup lịch khi `maxDate` (ngày sinh, ngày bắt đầu yêu) → không chọn được ngày tương lai. Test ở Task 3.
4. Lưu khoảnh khắc khi mạng lỗi giữa chừng (upload ảnh thứ 2 lỗi) → báo lỗi, giữ nguyên dữ liệu đã nhập, không tạo kỷ niệm thiếu ảnh. Test ở Task 10.
5. Deep link từ thông báo mới (`coupleapp://notes`, `coupleapp://pair/requests`, `coupleapp://moments/{id}`) → mở đúng màn. Test ở Task 15.

- [ ] **Step 3: Viết test primitives**

`mobile/src/ui/__tests__/ui-test.tsx` (thay toàn bộ):

```tsx
import { fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react-native';
import { Text } from 'react-native';

import { Button } from '@/ui/Button';
import { Chip } from '@/ui/Chip';
import { SegmentedTabs } from '@/ui/SegmentedTabs';
import { Sheet } from '@/ui/Sheet';
import { SocialButton } from '@/ui/SocialButton';
import { gradientFor } from '@/ui/theme';
import { Toggle } from '@/ui/Toggle';

describe('ui primitives', () => {
  test('button calls onPress and is disabled while loading', async () => {
    const onPress = jest.fn();
    const { rerender } = await render(<Button title="Lưu" onPress={onPress} />);
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Lưu' }));
    expect(onPress).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    await rerender(<Button title="Lưu" onPress={onPress} loading />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Lưu' })).toBeDisabled();
  });

  test('chip exposes selected state', async () => {
    await render(<Chip label="Nhớ nhung" selected onPress={jest.fn()} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Nhớ nhung' })).toBeSelected();
  });

  test('toggle is a switch that flips value', async () => {
    const onChange = jest.fn();
    await render(<Toggle label="Giữ bí mật" value={false} onValueChange={onChange} />);
    const sw = screen.getByRole('switch', { name: 'Giữ bí mật' });
    expect(sw).not.toBeChecked();
    await fireEvent.press(sw);
    expect(onChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith(true);
  });

  test('segmented tabs report the chosen key', async () => {
    const onChange = jest.fn();
    await render(
      <SegmentedTabs
        options={[{ key: 'grid', label: 'Lưới' }, { key: 'month', label: 'Tháng' }]}
        value="grid"
        onChange={onChange}
      />,
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Lưới' })).toBeSelected();
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Tháng' }));
    expect(onChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith('month');
  });

  test('sheet shows title and closes from the scrim', async () => {
    const onClose = jest.fn();
    await render(
      <Sheet visible title="Lặp lại" onClose={onClose}>
        <Text>Nội dung</Text>
      </Sheet>,
    );
    expect(screen.getByText('Lặp lại')).toBeOnTheScreen();
    expect(screen.getByText('Nội dung')).toBeOnTheScreen();
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByTestId('sheet-scrim'));
    expect(onClose).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  test('social button label', async () => {
    await render(<SocialButton provider="apple" onPress={jest.fn()} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Tiếp tục với Apple' })).toBeOnTheScreen();
  });

  test('gradientFor is stable per seed', () => {
    expect(gradientFor('abc')).toEqual(gradientFor('abc'));
  });
});
```

```ts
export const colors = {
  bg: '#FFF8F3',
  surface: '#FFFFFF',
  surfaceMuted: '#F6EEEA',
  line: '#EADFD9',
  text: '#2B1A2E',
  textMuted: '#6F5B6A',
  rose: '#E9557A',
  roseStrong: '#C9365C',
  roseSoft: '#FFE6EC',
  roseBorder: '#F2A3B6',
  peachSoft: '#FFEEDF',
  peachInk: '#B5562A',
  lavender: '#EEEAFF',
  lavenderInk: '#5B4BC4',
  mint: '#E3F5EC',
  mintInk: '#24795A',
  cycle: '#D64545',
  danger: '#C42F3F',
  scrim: 'rgba(43,26,46,0.45)',
  white: '#FFFFFF',
  toggleOff: '#D9CCC6',
  // Tên cũ — để màn hình chưa chuyển sang v2 vẫn chạy; xoá ở Task 15
  primary: '#C9365C',
  primaryDark: '#A82A4B',
  card: '#FFFFFF',
  muted: '#6F5B6A',
  border: '#EADFD9',
  success: '#24795A',
};

export const fonts = {
  regular: 'BeVietnamPro_400Regular',
  medium: 'BeVietnamPro_500Medium',
  semibold: 'BeVietnamPro_600SemiBold',
  bold: 'BeVietnamPro_700Bold',
  extrabold: 'BeVietnamPro_800ExtraBold',
};

export const text = {
  display: { fontFamily: fonts.extrabold, fontSize: 28, lineHeight: 35 },
  h1: { fontFamily: fonts.extrabold, fontSize: 26, lineHeight: 32 },
  h2: { fontFamily: fonts.bold, fontSize: 20, lineHeight: 26 },
  h3: { fontFamily: fonts.bold, fontSize: 17, lineHeight: 22 },
  body: { fontFamily: fonts.regular, fontSize: 15, lineHeight: 22 },
  bodyStrong: { fontFamily: fonts.bold, fontSize: 15, lineHeight: 22 },
  caption: { fontFamily: fonts.regular, fontSize: 13, lineHeight: 18 },
  captionStrong: { fontFamily: fonts.semibold, fontSize: 13, lineHeight: 18 },
  micro: { fontFamily: fonts.bold, fontSize: 11, lineHeight: 14, letterSpacing: 0.66 },
} as const;

export type TextVariant = keyof typeof text;

export const spacing = { xs: 4, sm: 8, md: 12, lg: 16, xl: 24 };
export const radius = { sm: 12, md: 16, lg: 20, xl: 24, sheet: 32, pill: 999 };

export const shadow = {
  card: { shadowColor: '#2B1A2E', shadowOpacity: 0.06, shadowRadius: 12, shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 8 }, elevation: 2 },
  raised: { shadowColor: '#2B1A2E', shadowOpacity: 0.12, shadowRadius: 18, shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 10 }, elevation: 5 },
};

export const GRADIENTS: [string, string][] = [
  ['#FFC8B4', '#F38BA8'],
  ['#C9D8FF', '#A99BF0'],
  ['#BFEBD9', '#7CC9AE'],
  ['#FFE1A8', '#FFAE8A'],
];

/** Gradient giữ chỗ cho ảnh, cố định theo id để không nhảy màu giữa các lần render. */
export function gradientFor(seed: string): [string, string] {
  let h = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < seed.length; i++) h = (h * 31 + seed.charCodeAt(i)) >>> 0;
  return GRADIENTS[h % GRADIENTS.length];
}
```

```tsx
// src/ui/Icon.tsx
import Feather from '@expo/vector-icons/Feather';
import Ionicons from '@expo/vector-icons/Ionicons';

import { colors } from './theme';

const FEATHER = {
  heart: 'heart', camera: 'camera', image: 'image', calendar: 'calendar', pin: 'map-pin', bell: 'bell',
  gift: 'gift', droplet: 'droplet', lock: 'lock', link: 'link', user: 'user', search: 'search', share: 'share-2',
  copy: 'copy', edit: 'edit-2', close: 'x', back: 'chevron-left', chevronRight: 'chevron-right',
  chevronDown: 'chevron-down', plus: 'plus', repeat: 'repeat', clock: 'clock', send: 'send', zap: 'zap',
  flip: 'refresh-cw', eye: 'eye', shield: 'shield', logout: 'log-out', layers: 'layers', journal: 'book-open',
  home: 'home', hash: 'hash', navigation: 'navigation', check: 'check', more: 'more-horizontal', type: 'type',
  film: 'film', smile: 'smile', trash: 'trash-2', slash: 'slash', pen: 'edit-3', quote: 'message-circle',
} as const;

const IONICONS = {
  sparkles: 'sparkles-outline', qr: 'qr-code-outline', heartFilled: 'heart', unlink: 'unlink-outline',
  stamp: 'ribbon-outline', bunny: 'paw-outline', apple: 'logo-apple', google: 'logo-google',
  facebook: 'logo-facebook',
} as const;

export type IconName = keyof typeof FEATHER | keyof typeof IONICONS;

export function Icon({ name, size = 20, color = colors.text }: { name: IconName; size?: number; color?: string }) {
  if (name in IONICONS) {
    return <Ionicons name={IONICONS[name as keyof typeof IONICONS]} size={size} color={color} />;
  }
  return <Feather name={FEATHER[name as keyof typeof FEATHER]} size={size} color={color} />;
}
```

```tsx
// src/ui/AppText.tsx
import { Text, TextProps } from 'react-native';

import { colors, text, TextVariant } from './theme';

export function AppText({ variant = 'body', color = colors.text, style, ...rest }: TextProps & { variant?: TextVariant; color?: string }) {
  return <Text {...rest} style={[text[variant], { color }, style]} />;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: `Button`, `Chip`, `Toggle`, `IconCircle`, `Avatar`, `Card`**

```tsx
// src/ui/Button.tsx
import { ActivityIndicator, Pressable, StyleProp, StyleSheet, ViewStyle } from 'react-native';

import { AppText } from './AppText';
import { Icon, IconName } from './Icon';
import { colors } from './theme';

type Variant = 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'ghost' | 'danger';

const VARIANTS: Record<Variant, { bg: string; fg: string; border: string }> = {
  primary: { bg: colors.roseStrong, fg: colors.white, border: colors.roseStrong },
  secondary: { bg: colors.surface, fg: colors.text, border: colors.line },
  ghost: { bg: 'transparent', fg: colors.roseStrong, border: 'transparent' },
  danger: { bg: colors.surface, fg: colors.danger, border: colors.line },
};

type Props = {
  title: string;
  onPress?: () => void;
  variant?: Variant;
  disabled?: boolean;
  loading?: boolean;
  icon?: IconName;
  compact?: boolean;
  testID?: string;
  style?: StyleProp<ViewStyle>;
};

export function Button({ title, onPress, variant = 'primary', disabled = false, loading = false, icon, compact, testID, style }: Props) {
  const v = VARIANTS[variant];
  const inactive = disabled || loading;
  return (
    <Pressable
      testID={testID}
      accessibilityRole="button"
      accessibilityLabel={title}
      accessibilityState={{ disabled: inactive, busy: loading }}
      disabled={inactive}
      onPress={onPress}
      style={({ pressed }) => [
        styles.base,
        compact && styles.compact,
        { backgroundColor: v.bg, borderColor: v.border },
        (pressed || inactive) && { opacity: inactive ? 0.5 : 0.85 },
        style,
      ]}>
      {loading ? (
        <ActivityIndicator color={v.fg} />
      ) : (
        <>
          {icon ? <Icon name={icon} size={18} color={v.fg} /> : null}
          <AppText variant="bodyStrong" color={v.fg} style={{ fontSize: compact ? 15 : 16 }}>
            {title}
          </AppText>
        </>
      )}
    </Pressable>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  base: { height: 54, borderRadius: 18, borderWidth: 1.5, flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 8, paddingHorizontal: 16 },
  compact: { height: 44, borderRadius: 16 },
});
```

```tsx
// src/ui/Chip.tsx
import { Pressable, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';

import { AppText } from './AppText';
import { colors } from './theme';

export function Chip({ label, selected, onPress, testID }: { label: string; selected: boolean; onPress: () => void; testID?: string }) {
  return (
    <Pressable
      testID={testID}
      accessibilityRole="button"
      accessibilityLabel={label}
      accessibilityState={{ selected }}
      onPress={onPress}
      style={[styles.chip, selected ? styles.on : styles.off]}>
      <AppText variant="captionStrong" color={selected ? colors.white : colors.text}>
        {label}
      </AppText>
    </Pressable>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  chip: { minHeight: 36, paddingHorizontal: 14, borderRadius: 999, borderWidth: 1.5, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  on: { backgroundColor: colors.roseStrong, borderColor: colors.roseStrong },
  off: { backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderColor: colors.line },
});
```

```tsx
// src/ui/Toggle.tsx
import { Pressable, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';

import { colors } from './theme';

export function Toggle({ value, onValueChange, label }: { value: boolean; onValueChange: (v: boolean) => void; label: string }) {
  return (
    <Pressable
      accessibilityRole="switch"
      accessibilityLabel={label}
      accessibilityState={{ checked: value }}
      hitSlop={8}
      onPress={() => onValueChange(!value)}
      style={[styles.track, { backgroundColor: value ? colors.roseStrong : colors.toggleOff }]}>
      <View style={[styles.knob, { left: value ? 24 : 4 }]} />
    </Pressable>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  track: { width: 50, height: 30, borderRadius: 15 },
  knob: { position: 'absolute', top: 4, width: 22, height: 22, borderRadius: 11, backgroundColor: colors.white },
});
```

```tsx
// src/ui/IconCircle.tsx
import { View } from 'react-native';

import { Icon, IconName } from './Icon';

export function IconCircle({ name, bg, color, size = 40 }: { name: IconName; bg: string; color: string; size?: number }) {
  return (
    <View style={{ width: size, height: size, borderRadius: size / 2, backgroundColor: bg, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
      <Icon name={name} size={Math.round(size / 2)} color={color} />
    </View>
  );
}
```

```tsx
// src/ui/Avatar.tsx
import { Image } from 'expo-image';
import { View } from 'react-native';

import { AppText } from './AppText';
import { colors } from './theme';

const TONES = {
  rose: { bg: colors.roseSoft, fg: colors.roseStrong },
  lavender: { bg: colors.lavender, fg: colors.lavenderInk },
  peach: { bg: colors.peachSoft, fg: colors.peachInk },
};

type Props = { name: string; tone?: keyof typeof TONES; size?: number; uri?: string | null; ring?: boolean };

export function Avatar({ name, tone = 'rose', size = 48, uri, ring }: Props) {
  const t = TONES[tone];
  const box = { width: size, height: size, borderRadius: size / 2, borderWidth: ring ? 3 : 0, borderColor: colors.white };
  if (uri) {
    return <Image source={{ uri }} style={box} contentFit="cover" accessibilityLabel={name} />;
  }
  return (
    <View accessibilityLabel={name} style={[box, { backgroundColor: t.bg, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }]}>
      <AppText variant="bodyStrong" color={t.fg} style={{ fontSize: Math.round(size * 0.38), lineHeight: Math.round(size * 0.5) }}>
        {name.trim().charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
      </AppText>
    </View>
  );
}
```

```tsx
// src/ui/ListRow.tsx
import { Pressable, View } from 'react-native';

import { AppText } from './AppText';
import { Icon, IconName } from './Icon';
import { IconCircle } from './IconCircle';
import { colors } from './theme';

type Props = {
  icon: IconName;
  iconBg: string;
  iconColor: string;
  title: string;
  subtitle?: string | null;
  right?: React.ReactNode;
  onPress?: () => void;
  danger?: boolean;
  testID?: string;
};

export function ListRow({ icon, iconBg, iconColor, title, subtitle, right, onPress, danger, testID }: Props) {
  const body = (
    <>
      <IconCircle name={icon} bg={iconBg} color={iconColor} size={36} />
      <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
        <AppText variant="bodyStrong" color={danger ? colors.danger : colors.text}>{title}</AppText>
        {subtitle ? <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted}>{subtitle}</AppText> : null}
      </View>
      {right ?? (onPress ? <Icon name="chevronRight" size={18} color={colors.textMuted} /> : null)}
    </>
  );
  const style = { flexDirection: 'row' as const, alignItems: 'center' as const, gap: 12, paddingVertical: 10 };
  return onPress ? (
    <Pressable testID={testID} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel={title} onPress={onPress} style={style}>
      {body}
    </Pressable>
  ) : (
    <View testID={testID} style={style}>{body}</View>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { StyleSheet, TextInput, TextInputProps, View } from 'react-native';

import { AppText } from './AppText';
import { colors, fonts } from './theme';

type Props = TextInputProps & { label: string; error?: string | null };

export function TextField({ label, error, style, multiline, ...input }: Props) {
  return (
    <View style={{ gap: 6 }}>
      <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted}>{label}</AppText>
      <TextInput
        accessibilityLabel={label}
        placeholderTextColor={colors.textMuted}
        multiline={multiline}
        style={[styles.input, multiline && styles.multi, !!error && { borderColor: colors.danger }, style]}
        {...input}
      />
      {error ? <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.danger}>{error}</AppText> : null}
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  input: { minHeight: 48, borderRadius: 16, borderWidth: 1.5, borderColor: colors.line, backgroundColor: colors.surface, paddingHorizontal: 14, fontFamily: fonts.regular, fontSize: 15, color: colors.text },
  multi: { minHeight: 76, paddingTop: 12, textAlignVertical: 'top' },
});
```

```tsx
import { colors } from './theme';

type Props = { title?: string; onBack?: () => void; backIcon?: 'back' | 'close'; right?: React.ReactNode };

export function TopBar({ title, onBack, backIcon = 'back', right }: Props) {

      <Pressable
        accessibilityRole="button"

        onPress={onBack ?? (() => router.back())}
        style={{ width: 44, height: 44, borderRadius: 22, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.line, backgroundColor: colors.surface, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
        <Icon name={backIcon} size={20} />
      </Pressable>
      <AppText variant="h3" style={{ flex: 1 }} numberOfLines={1}>{title ?? ''}</AppText>
      {right}
    </View>
  );
}
```

```tsx
// src/ui/SegmentedTabs.tsx
import { Pressable, View } from 'react-native';

import { AppText } from './AppText';
import { colors, shadow } from './theme';

type Props<T extends string> = { options: { key: T; label: string }[]; value: T; onChange: (key: T) => void };

export function SegmentedTabs<T extends string>({ options, value, onChange }: Props<T>) {
  return (
    <View accessibilityRole="tablist" style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 4, padding: 4, borderRadius: 16, backgroundColor: colors.surfaceMuted }}>
      {options.map((o) => {
        const on = o.key === value;
        return (
          <Pressable
            key={o.key}
            accessibilityRole="tab"
            accessibilityLabel={o.label}
            accessibilityState={{ selected: on }}
            onPress={() => onChange(o.key)}
            style={[{ flex: 1, height: 40, borderRadius: 12, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }, on && { backgroundColor: colors.surface, ...shadow.card }]}>
            <AppText variant={on ? 'bodyStrong' : 'captionStrong'} color={on ? colors.text : colors.textMuted}>{o.label}</AppText>
          </Pressable>
        );
      })}
    </View>
  );
}
```

```tsx
// src/ui/Sheet.tsx
import { KeyboardAvoidingView, Modal, Platform, Pressable, ScrollView, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';

import { AppText } from './AppText';
import { colors } from './theme';

type Props = {
  visible: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  title?: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  right?: React.ReactNode;
  children: React.ReactNode;
  testID?: string;
};

export function Sheet({ visible, onClose, title, subtitle, right, children, testID }: Props) {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  return (
    <Modal visible={visible} transparent animationType="slide" statusBarTranslucent onRequestClose={onClose}>
      <KeyboardAvoidingView behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined} style={styles.root}>
        <Pressable testID="sheet-scrim" accessibilityLabel="Đóng" style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} onPress={onClose} />
        <View testID={testID} style={[styles.sheet, { paddingBottom: Math.max(insets.bottom, 16) + 18 }]}>
          <View style={styles.grabber} />
          {title ? (
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12 }}>
              <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                <AppText variant="h2">{title}</AppText>
                {subtitle ? <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted}>{subtitle}</AppText> : null}
              </View>
              {right}
            </View>
          ) : null}
          <ScrollView bounces={false} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled" contentContainerStyle={{ gap: 14 }}>
            {children}
          </ScrollView>
        </View>
      </KeyboardAvoidingView>
    </Modal>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, justifyContent: 'flex-end', backgroundColor: colors.scrim },
  sheet: { maxHeight: '92%', backgroundColor: colors.bg, borderTopLeftRadius: 32, borderTopRightRadius: 32, paddingHorizontal: 24, paddingTop: 12, gap: 14 },
  grabber: { alignSelf: 'center', width: 40, height: 5, borderRadius: 3, backgroundColor: colors.line },
});
```

```tsx
import { Pressable } from 'react-native';

import { AppText } from './AppText';
import { Icon } from './Icon';
import { colors } from './theme';

const PROVIDERS = {
  apple: { label: 'Apple', bg: '#000000', fg: colors.white, iconColor: colors.white, border: '#000000' },
  google: { label: 'Google', bg: colors.surface, fg: colors.text, iconColor: '#4285F4', border: colors.line },
  facebook: { label: 'Facebook', bg: colors.surface, fg: colors.text, iconColor: '#1877F2', border: colors.line },
} as const;

export function SocialButton({ provider, onPress }: { provider: keyof typeof PROVIDERS; onPress: () => void }) {
  const p = PROVIDERS[provider];
  const label = `Tiếp tục với ${p.label}`;
  return (
    <Pressable
      accessibilityRole="button"
      accessibilityLabel={label}
      onPress={onPress}
      style={{ height: 52, borderRadius: 18, borderWidth: 1.5, borderColor: p.border, backgroundColor: p.bg, flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 10 }}>
      <Icon name={provider} size={20} color={p.iconColor} />
      <AppText variant="bodyStrong" color={p.fg}>{label}</AppText>
    </Pressable>
  );
}
```

```tsx
// src/ui/PhotoTile.tsx
import { Image } from 'expo-image';
import { LinearGradient } from 'expo-linear-gradient';
import { StyleProp, View, ViewStyle } from 'react-native';

import { gradientFor } from './theme';

type Props = { uri?: string | null; seed: string; style?: StyleProp<ViewStyle>; children?: React.ReactNode; testID?: string };

/** Ảnh thật nếu có, nếu không là gradient giữ chỗ cố định theo `seed`. */
export function PhotoTile({ uri, seed, style, children, testID }: Props) {
  return (
    <View testID={testID} style={[{ overflow: 'hidden', borderRadius: 20 }, style]}>
      {uri ? (
        <Image source={{ uri }} style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 0, left: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0 }} contentFit="cover" />
      ) : (
        <LinearGradient colors={gradientFor(seed)} start={{ x: 0, y: 0 }} end={{ x: 1, y: 1 }} style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 0, left: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0 }} />
      )}
      {children}
    </View>
  );
}
```

```tsx
// src/ui/Screen.tsx
import { ScrollView, View } from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';

import { colors } from './theme';

type Props = { children: React.ReactNode; scroll?: boolean; padded?: boolean; bottomPad?: number; gap?: number };

export function Screen({ children, scroll = true, padded = true, bottomPad = 24, gap = 14 }: Props) {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const content = { paddingTop: insets.top + 8, paddingHorizontal: padded ? 24 : 0, paddingBottom: bottomPad, gap };
  return scroll ? (
    <ScrollView style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.bg }} contentContainerStyle={content} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
      {children}
    </ScrollView>
  ) : (
    <View style={[{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.bg }, content]}>{children}</View>
  );
}
```

```tsx
export function ErrorText({ message }: { message?: string | null }) {

  return <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.danger} accessibilityRole="alert">{message}</AppText>;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Viết test hook**

`mobile/src/features/inbox/__tests__/api-hooks-test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { renderHook, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react-native';

import { useAcceptPairRequest } from '@/features/couple/api';
import { useUnreadCount } from '@/features/inbox/api';
import { useLatestNote, useSendNote } from '@/features/notes/api';
import { keys } from '@/lib/query';
import { mockApi } from '@/test/api-mock';

function setup() {
  const client = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false, gcTime: Infinity }, mutations: { retry: false, gcTime: Infinity } } });
  const wrapper = ({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) => <QueryClientProvider client={client}>{children}</QueryClientProvider>;
  return { client, wrapper };
}

const COUPLE = {
  id: 'c1', startedDatingOn: '2025-02-14', createdAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z',
  partner: { id: 'u1', displayName: 'Lan', handle: 'lan.2001', birthday: null, avatarUrl: null },
};

describe('api hooks', () => {
  test('latest note is null when backend answers 204', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'GET /api/love-notes/latest': { status: 204 } });
    const { wrapper } = setup();
    const { result } = await renderHook(() => useLatestNote(), { wrapper });
    await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.isSuccess).toBe(true));
    expect(result.current.data).toBeNull();
  });

  test('sending a note posts the body and refreshes the list', async () => {
    const api = mockApi({
      'POST /api/love-notes': { status: 201, body: { id: 'n1', authorId: 'u2', kind: 'TEXT', text: 'Nhớ anh', drawing: null, seenAt: null, createdAt: '2026-10-11T00:00:00Z' } },
    });
    const { wrapper, client } = setup();
    const spy = jest.spyOn(client, 'invalidateQueries');
    const { result } = await renderHook(() => useSendNote(), { wrapper });
    await result.current.mutateAsync({ kind: 'TEXT', text: 'Nhớ anh' });
    expect(api.calls.find((c) => c.method === 'POST')?.body).toEqual({ kind: 'TEXT', text: 'Nhớ anh' });
    expect(spy).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ queryKey: keys.notes });
  });

  test('unread count reads the count field', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'GET /api/notifications/unread-count': { body: { count: 3 } } });
    const { wrapper } = setup();
    const { result } = await renderHook(() => useUnreadCount(), { wrapper });
    await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.data).toBe(3));
  });

  test('accepting a pair request stores the new couple', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'POST /api/couples/requests/r1/accept': { status: 201, body: COUPLE } });
    const { wrapper, client } = setup();
    const { result } = await renderHook(() => useAcceptPairRequest(), { wrapper });
    await result.current.mutateAsync({ id: 'r1', startedDatingOn: '2025-02-14' });
    expect(client.getQueryData(keys.couple)).toEqual(COUPLE);
  });
});
```

```ts
export type Me = {
  id: string;
  email: string;
  displayName: string;
  handle: string;

};

export type MomentType = 'ANNIVERSARY' | 'DATE_PLAN' | 'GIFT_OCCASION' | 'CYCLE' | 'CUSTOM' | 'MOMENT';
```

```ts
};
```

```ts
export const MOODS: { code: string; label: string }[] = [
  { code: 'MISSING', label: 'Nhớ nhung' },
  { code: 'HAPPY', label: 'Hạnh phúc' },
  { code: 'PEACEFUL', label: 'Bình yên' },
  { code: 'GRATEFUL', label: 'Biết ơn' },
  { code: 'SAD', label: 'Buồn một chút' },
  { code: 'OTHER', label: 'Khác' },
];

export function moodLabel(code: unknown): string | null {
  return MOODS.find((m) => m.code === code)?.label ?? null;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Hook lời nhắn & hộp thông báo**

`src/features/notes/api.ts`:

```ts
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';

import { apiFetch } from '@/lib/api/client';
import type { LoveNote } from '@/lib/api/types';
import { keys } from '@/lib/query';

export function useLoveNotes() {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: keys.notes, queryFn: () => apiFetch<LoveNote[]>('/api/love-notes?limit=50') });
}

/** Lời nhắn mới nhất người ấy gửi; backend trả 204 khi chưa có. */
export function useLatestNote(enabled = true) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.latestNote,
    queryFn: async () => (await apiFetch<LoveNote | undefined>('/api/love-notes/latest')) ?? null,
    enabled,
  });
}

export type SendNote = { kind: 'TEXT'; text: string } | { kind: 'DRAWING'; drawingMediaId: string; text?: string };

export function useSendNote() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: SendNote) => apiFetch<LoveNote>('/api/love-notes', { method: 'POST', body }),
    onSuccess: () => qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: keys.notes }),
  });
}

export function useMarkNoteSeen() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => apiFetch<void>(`/api/love-notes/${id}/seen`, { method: 'POST' }),
    onSuccess: () => qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: keys.notes }),
  });
}
```

(Nếu `apiFetch` ném lỗi khi body rỗng thay vì trả `undefined`, sửa `parse` trong `client.ts` để `text === ''` → trả `undefined as T`; test Step 1 kiểm điều này.)

`src/features/inbox/api.ts`:

```ts
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';

import { apiFetch } from '@/lib/api/client';
import type { InboxItem } from '@/lib/api/types';
import { keys } from '@/lib/query';

export function useInbox() {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: keys.inbox, queryFn: () => apiFetch<InboxItem[]>('/api/notifications?limit=50') });
}

export function useUnreadCount() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.unread,
    queryFn: async () => (await apiFetch<{ count: number }>('/api/notifications/unread-count')).count,
    refetchInterval: 60_000,
  });
}

export function useMarkRead() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => apiFetch<void>(`/api/notifications/${id}/read`, { method: 'POST' }),
    onSuccess: () => qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: keys.inbox }),
  });
}

export function useMarkAllRead() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => apiFetch<void>('/api/notifications/read-all', { method: 'POST' }),
    onSuccess: () => qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: keys.inbox }),
  });
}
```

```ts
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (handle: string) => apiFetch('/api/couples/requests', { method: 'POST', body: { handle } }),
    onSuccess: () => qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: keys.pairRequests }),
  });
}

export function useAcceptPairRequest() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ id, startedDatingOn }: { id: string; startedDatingOn?: string }) =>
      apiFetch<Couple>(`/api/couples/requests/${id}/accept`, { method: 'POST', body: startedDatingOn ? { startedDatingOn } : {} }),
    onSuccess: (couple) => {
      qc.setQueryData(keys.couple, couple);
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: keys.pairRequests });
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: keys.moments });
    },
  });
}

export function useDeclinePairRequest() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => apiFetch<void>(`/api/couples/requests/${id}/decline`, { method: 'POST' }),
    onSuccess: () => qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: keys.pairRequests }),
  });
}

export function useCancelPairRequest() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => apiFetch<void>(`/api/couples/requests/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' }),
    onSuccess: () => qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: keys.pairRequests }),
  });
}
```

```ts
import { dayMonth, monthGrid, monthLabel, shiftMonth, weekdayName } from '@/lib/calendar';

describe('calendar', () => {
  test('February 2025 starts on Saturday and has 5 rows', () => {
    const cells = monthGrid(2025, 2);
    expect(cells).toHaveLength(35);
    expect(cells.slice(0, 5).every((c) => c.day === null)).toBe(true);
    expect(cells[5]).toEqual({ iso: '2025-02-01', day: 1, weekday: 5 });
    expect(cells.filter((c) => c.day !== null)).toHaveLength(28);

  test('March 2001 starts on Thursday', () => {
    expect(monthGrid(2001, 3)[3].iso).toBe('2001-03-01');
  });

  test('shiftMonth wraps across years', () => {
    expect(shiftMonth(2026, 12, 1)).toEqual({ year: 2027, month: 1 });

    expect(weekdayName('2026-10-11')).toBe('Chủ Nhật');
    expect(dayMonth('2026-03-08')).toBe('08/03');

});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `src/lib/calendar.ts`**

```ts
import { pad, parseIsoDate, toIsoDate } from './format';

export type DayCell = { iso: string | null; day: number | null; weekday: number };

export const WEEKDAYS = ['T2', 'T3', 'T4', 'T5', 'T6', 'T7', 'CN'];
const WEEKDAY_NAMES = ['Thứ Hai', 'Thứ Ba', 'Thứ Tư', 'Thứ Năm', 'Thứ Sáu', 'Thứ Bảy', 'Chủ Nhật'];

/** Lưới tháng bắt đầu từ Thứ Hai, đủ số tuần (5 hoặc 6 hàng). month: 1–12. */
export function monthGrid(year: number, month: number): DayCell[] {
  const offset = (new Date(year, month - 1, 1).getDay() + 6) % 7;
  const days = new Date(year, month, 0).getDate();
  const total = Math.ceil((offset + days) / 7) * 7;
  return Array.from({ length: total }, (_, i) => {
    const d = i - offset + 1;
    const inMonth = d >= 1 && d <= days;
    return { iso: inMonth ? toIsoDate(new Date(year, month - 1, d)) : null, day: inMonth ? d : null, weekday: i % 7 };
  });
}

export function shiftMonth(year: number, month: number, delta: number): { year: number; month: number } {
  const d = new Date(year, month - 1 + delta, 1);
  return { year: d.getFullYear(), month: d.getMonth() + 1 };
}

export function monthLabel(year: number, month: number): string {
  return `Tháng ${month}, ${year}`;
}

/** 0 = Thứ Hai … 6 = Chủ Nhật. */
export function weekdayIndex(iso: string): number {
  return (parseIsoDate(iso).getDay() + 6) % 7;
}

export function weekdayName(iso: string): string {
  return WEEKDAY_NAMES[weekdayIndex(iso)];
}

export function dayMonth(iso: string): string {
  const d = parseIsoDate(iso);
  return `${pad(d.getDate())}/${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}`;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: `src/ui/DatePickerSheet.tsx`**

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Pressable, StyleSheet, TextInput, View } from 'react-native';

import { monthGrid, monthLabel, shiftMonth, WEEKDAYS } from '@/lib/calendar';
import { pad, toIsoDate } from '@/lib/format';

import { AppText } from './AppText';
import { Button } from './Button';
import { Chip } from './Chip';
import { Icon } from './Icon';
import { Sheet } from './Sheet';
import { colors, fonts, shadow } from './theme';

type Props = {
  visible: boolean;
  title: string;
  value: string | null;
  mode?: 'date' | 'datetime' | 'time';
  time?: string | null;
  quick?: boolean;
  maxDate?: string;
  onConfirm: (date: string, time: string | null) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
};

export function DatePickerSheet({ visible, title, value, mode = 'date', time, quick = true, maxDate, onConfirm, onClose }: Props) {
  const today = toIsoDate(new Date());
  const [sel, setSel] = useState(value ?? today);
  const [view, setView] = useState({ year: 0, month: 0 });
  const [hour, setHour] = useState('19');
  const [minute, setMinute] = useState('00');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!visible) return;
    const start = value ?? today;
    setSel(start);
    setView({ year: Number(start.slice(0, 4)), month: Number(start.slice(5, 7)) });
    setHour(time?.slice(0, 2) ?? '19');
    setMinute(time?.slice(3, 5) ?? '00');
    setError(null);
  }, [visible, value, time, today]);

  function pick(iso: string) {
    if (maxDate && iso > maxDate) return;
    setSel(iso);
    setView({ year: Number(iso.slice(0, 4)), month: Number(iso.slice(5, 7)) });
  }

  function confirm() {
    if (mode === 'date') {
      onConfirm(sel, null);
      return;
    }
    const h = Number(hour);
    const m = Number(minute);
    if (!/^\d{1,2}$/.test(hour) || !/^\d{1,2}$/.test(minute) || h > 23 || m > 59) {
      setError('Giờ không hợp lệ');
      return;
    }
    onConfirm(sel, `${pad(h)}:${pad(m)}`);
  }

  const firstOfMonth = `${today.slice(0, 8)}01`;
  const yesterday = toIsoDate(new Date(Date.now() - 86_400_000));

  return (
    <Sheet visible={visible} onClose={onClose} title={title}>
      {mode !== 'time' ? (
        <>
          <View style={styles.nav}>
            <View style={styles.monthChip}>
              <AppText variant="bodyStrong">{monthLabel(view.year, view.month)}</AppText>
            </View>
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8 }}>
              <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Tháng trước" style={styles.arrow}
                onPress={() => setView(shiftMonth(view.year, view.month, -1))}>
                <Icon name="back" size={18} />
              </Pressable>
              <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Tháng sau" style={styles.arrow}
                onPress={() => setView(shiftMonth(view.year, view.month, 1))}>
                <Icon name="chevronRight" size={18} />
              </Pressable>
            </View>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.grid}>
            {WEEKDAYS.map((w) => (
              <AppText key={w} variant="micro" color={w === 'CN' ? colors.roseStrong : colors.textMuted} style={styles.weekday}>{w}</AppText>
            ))}
            {monthGrid(view.year, view.month).map((c, i) => {
              if (!c.iso) return <View key={`e${i}`} style={styles.cell} />;
              const on = c.iso === sel;
              const disabled = !!maxDate && c.iso > maxDate;
              return (
                <View key={c.iso} style={styles.cell}>
                  <Pressable
                    accessibilityRole="button"
                    accessibilityLabel={`Ngày ${c.day}`}
                    accessibilityState={{ selected: on, disabled }}
                    disabled={disabled}
                    onPress={() => pick(c.iso!)}
                    style={[styles.day, on && styles.dayOn, disabled && { opacity: 0.3 }]}>
                    <AppText variant={on ? 'bodyStrong' : 'body'} color={on ? colors.white : c.weekday === 6 ? colors.roseStrong : colors.text}>
                      {c.day}
                    </AppText>
                  </Pressable>
                </View>
              );
            })}
          </View>
        </>
      ) : null}

      {mode !== 'date' ? (
        <View style={styles.timeRow}>
          <Icon name="clock" size={20} color={colors.textMuted} />
          <AppText variant="bodyStrong" style={{ flex: 1 }}>Giờ</AppText>
          <TextInput accessibilityLabel="Giờ" value={hour} onChangeText={setHour} keyboardType="number-pad" maxLength={2} style={styles.timeBox} />
          <AppText variant="h2">:</AppText>
          <TextInput accessibilityLabel="Phút" value={minute} onChangeText={setMinute} keyboardType="number-pad" maxLength={2} style={styles.timeBox} />
        </View>
      ) : null}
      {error ? <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.danger}>{error}</AppText> : null}

      {quick && mode !== 'time' ? (
        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8 }}>
          <Chip label="Hôm nay" selected={sel === today} onPress={() => pick(today)} />
          <Chip label="Hôm qua" selected={sel === yesterday} onPress={() => pick(yesterday)} />
          <Chip label="Đầu tháng này" selected={sel === firstOfMonth} onPress={() => pick(firstOfMonth)} />
        </View>
      ) : null}

      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 10 }}>
        <Button title="Huỷ" variant="secondary" onPress={onClose} style={{ flex: 1 }} />
        <Button title="Xác nhận" onPress={confirm} style={{ flex: 1 }} />
      </View>
    </Sheet>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  nav: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between' },
  monthChip: { height: 38, paddingHorizontal: 14, borderRadius: 999, backgroundColor: colors.surfaceMuted, justifyContent: 'center' },
  arrow: { width: 40, height: 40, borderRadius: 20, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.line, backgroundColor: colors.surface, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  grid: { flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderRadius: 24, paddingVertical: 12, paddingHorizontal: 8, ...shadow.card },
  weekday: { width: `${100 / 7}%`, textAlign: 'center', paddingBottom: 6 },
  cell: { width: `${100 / 7}%`, height: 42, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  day: { width: 38, height: 38, borderRadius: 19, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  dayOn: { backgroundColor: colors.roseStrong },
  timeRow: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, padding: 14, borderRadius: 20, backgroundColor: colors.surface },
  timeBox: { width: 54, height: 42, borderRadius: 12, backgroundColor: colors.roseSoft, textAlign: 'center', fontFamily: fonts.bold, fontSize: 20, color: colors.roseStrong },
});
```

```tsx
import { renderHook } from '@testing-library/react-native';
import { router } from 'expo-router';

import { useOnboardingRedirect } from '@/lib/use-onboarding';

const finish = jest.fn();
let onboarding = true;
jest.mock('@/lib/auth/session', () => ({
  useSession: () => ({ onboarding, finishOnboarding: finish }),
}));

describe('useOnboardingRedirect', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    onboarding = true;
  });

  test('new unpaired user is sent to pairing once', async () => {
    await renderHook(() => useOnboardingRedirect(false));
    expect(router.push).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/pair');
    expect(finish).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  test('waits while couple is loading and skips paired users', async () => {
    await renderHook(() => useOnboardingRedirect(undefined));
    await renderHook(() => useOnboardingRedirect(true));
    expect(router.push).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  test('returning users are not redirected', async () => {
    onboarding = false;
    await renderHook(() => useOnboardingRedirect(false));
    expect(router.push).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `src/ui/TabBar.tsx`**

```tsx
import { Pressable, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';

import { AppText } from './AppText';
import { Icon, IconName } from './Icon';
import { colors } from './theme';

export type TabName = 'index' | 'journal' | 'create' | 'partner' | 'me';

const TABS: { name: TabName; label: string; icon: IconName }[] = [
  { name: 'index', label: 'Trang chủ', icon: 'home' },
  { name: 'journal', label: 'Nhật ký', icon: 'journal' },
  { name: 'create', label: 'Tạo mới', icon: 'plus' },
  { name: 'partner', label: 'Người ấy', icon: 'heart' },
  { name: 'me', label: 'Tôi', icon: 'user' },
];

export function TabBar({ active, onSelect }: { active: string; onSelect: (name: TabName) => void }) {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  return (
    <View style={[styles.bar, { paddingBottom: Math.max(insets.bottom, 12) }]}>
      {TABS.map((t) => {
        const on = t.name === active;
        if (t.name === 'create') {
          return (
            <Pressable key={t.name} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel={t.label} accessibilityState={{ selected: on }}
              onPress={() => onSelect(t.name)} style={[styles.plus, on && { backgroundColor: colors.text }]}>
              <Icon name="plus" size={26} color={colors.white} />
            </Pressable>
          );
        }
        return (
          <Pressable key={t.name} accessibilityRole="tab" accessibilityLabel={t.label} accessibilityState={{ selected: on }}
            onPress={() => onSelect(t.name)} style={styles.tab}>
            <Icon name={t.icon} size={24} color={on ? colors.roseStrong : colors.textMuted} />
            <AppText variant="micro" color={on ? colors.roseStrong : colors.textMuted} style={{ letterSpacing: 0 }}>{t.label}</AppText>
          </Pressable>
        );
      })}
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  bar: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-around', paddingTop: 10, paddingHorizontal: 12, backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderTopWidth: 1, borderTopColor: colors.line },
  tab: { width: 64, alignItems: 'center', gap: 4, paddingVertical: 4 },
  plus: { width: 54, height: 54, borderRadius: 27, backgroundColor: colors.roseStrong, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', shadowColor: colors.roseStrong, shadowOpacity: 0.35, shadowRadius: 10, shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 6 }, elevation: 6 },
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Cờ onboarding trong session & hook chuyển hướng**

`session.tsx` — thêm vào `SessionValue`: `onboarding: boolean; finishOnboarding: () => void;`. Trong provider: `const [onboarding, setOnboarding] = useState(false);`. `signUp` thành:

```tsx
    signUp: async (email, password, displayName) => {
      await start('/api/auth/register', { email, password, displayName });
      setOnboarding(true);
    },
```

và thêm vào `value`: `onboarding, finishOnboarding: () => setOnboarding(false),`. `signOut` cũng gọi `setOnboarding(false)`.

`src/lib/use-onboarding.ts`:

```ts
import { router } from 'expo-router';
import { useEffect } from 'react';

import { useSession } from '@/lib/auth/session';

/** Vừa đăng ký và chưa có cặp → mở màn ghép đôi một lần (A2 → A3 trong mock). */
export function useOnboardingRedirect(paired: boolean | undefined) {
  const { onboarding, finishOnboarding } = useSession();
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!onboarding || paired === undefined) return;
    finishOnboarding();
    if (!paired) router.push('/pair');
  }, [onboarding, paired, finishOnboarding]);
}
```

```tsx
import { Stack } from 'expo-router';
import { ActivityIndicator, View } from 'react-native';

import { useCouple } from '@/features/couple/api';
import { errorMessage } from '@/lib/api/client';
import { useOnboardingRedirect } from '@/lib/use-onboarding';
import { useNotificationRouting, usePushRegistration } from '@/lib/use-push';
import { Button } from '@/ui/Button';
import { ErrorText } from '@/ui/ErrorText';
import { Screen } from '@/ui/Screen';
import { colors } from '@/ui/theme';

export default function AppLayout() {
  const couple = useCouple();
  usePushRegistration();
  useNotificationRouting();
  useOnboardingRedirect(couple.isSuccess ? couple.data !== null : undefined);

  if (couple.isPending) {
    return (
      <View style={{ flex: 1, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', backgroundColor: colors.bg }}>
        <ActivityIndicator color={colors.roseStrong} />
      </View>
    );
  }
  if (couple.isError) {
    return (
      <Screen>
        <ErrorText message={errorMessage(couple.error)} />
        <Button title="Thử lại" onPress={() => couple.refetch()} />
      </Screen>
    );
  }
  return <Stack screenOptions={{ headerShown: false }} />;
}
```

```ts
// src/features/account/social.ts
import { Alert } from 'react-native';

const LABELS = { apple: 'Apple', google: 'Google', facebook: 'Facebook' } as const;

/** Backend chưa hỗ trợ đăng nhập mạng xã hội (spec §12.1) — chỉ báo cho người dùng. */
export function socialComingSoon(provider: keyof typeof LABELS) {
  Alert.alert('Sắp ra mắt', `Đăng nhập bằng ${LABELS[provider]} sẽ có trong bản cập nhật tới. Hiện bạn dùng email và mật khẩu nhé.`);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `SignUpScreen.tsx` (A2)**

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { View } from 'react-native';

import { errorMessage } from '@/lib/api/client';
import { useSession } from '@/lib/auth/session';
import { AppText } from '@/ui/AppText';
import { Button } from '@/ui/Button';
import { ErrorText } from '@/ui/ErrorText';
import { Screen } from '@/ui/Screen';
import { SocialButton } from '@/ui/SocialButton';
import { TextField } from '@/ui/TextField';
import { colors } from '@/ui/theme';
import { TopBar } from '@/ui/TopBar';

import { socialComingSoon } from './social';

export function validateSignUp(name: string, email: string, password: string): string | null {
  if (!name.trim()) return 'Nhập tên hiển thị';
  if (!/^\S+@\S+\.\S+$/.test(email.trim())) return 'Email không hợp lệ';
  if (password.length < 8) return 'Mật khẩu tối thiểu 8 ký tự';
  return null;
}

export function OrDivider({ label }: { label: string }) {
  return (
    <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12 }}>
      <View style={{ flex: 1, height: 1, backgroundColor: colors.line }} />
      <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted}>{label}</AppText>
      <View style={{ flex: 1, height: 1, backgroundColor: colors.line }} />
    </View>
  );
}

export default function SignUpScreen() {
  const { signUp } = useSession();
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  async function submit() {
    const problem = validateSignUp(name, email, password);
    if (problem) {
      setError(problem);
      return;
    }
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await signUp(email.trim(), password, name.trim());
    } catch (e) {
      setError(errorMessage(e));
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <Screen gap={12}>
      <TopBar right={<AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted}>Bước 1/2</AppText>} />
      <View style={{ gap: 6, marginBottom: 4 }}>
        <AppText variant="display">Tạo tài khoản</AppText>
        <AppText color={colors.textMuted}>Đăng ký nhanh bằng tài khoản có sẵn.</AppText>
      </View>
      <SocialButton provider="apple" onPress={() => socialComingSoon('apple')} />
      <SocialButton provider="google" onPress={() => socialComingSoon('google')} />
      <SocialButton provider="facebook" onPress={() => socialComingSoon('facebook')} />
      <OrDivider label="hoặc dùng email" />
      <TextField label="Tên hiển thị" value={name} onChangeText={setName} />
      <TextField label="Email" value={email} onChangeText={setEmail} autoCapitalize="none" keyboardType="email-address" />
      <TextField label="Mật khẩu" value={password} onChangeText={setPassword} secureTextEntry />
      <ErrorText message={error} />
      <Button title="Tạo tài khoản" onPress={submit} loading={loading} />
    </Screen>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Alert, Pressable, View } from 'react-native';

import { errorMessage } from '@/lib/api/client';
import { useSession } from '@/lib/auth/session';
import { AppText } from '@/ui/AppText';
import { Button } from '@/ui/Button';
import { ErrorText } from '@/ui/ErrorText';
import { Screen } from '@/ui/Screen';
import { SocialButton } from '@/ui/SocialButton';
import { TextField } from '@/ui/TextField';
import { colors } from '@/ui/theme';
import { TopBar } from '@/ui/TopBar';

import { socialComingSoon } from './social';
import { OrDivider } from './SignUpScreen';

export default function SignInScreen() {
  const { signIn } = useSession();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  async function submit() {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await signIn(email.trim(), password);
    } catch (e) {
      setError(errorMessage(e));
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <Screen gap={12}>
      <TopBar />
      <View style={{ gap: 6, marginBottom: 4 }}>
        <AppText variant="display">Chào mừng trở lại</AppText>
        <AppText color={colors.textMuted}>Đăng nhập để xem người ấy có gì mới.</AppText>
      </View>
      <SocialButton provider="apple" onPress={() => socialComingSoon('apple')} />
      <SocialButton provider="google" onPress={() => socialComingSoon('google')} />
      <SocialButton provider="facebook" onPress={() => socialComingSoon('facebook')} />
      <OrDivider label="hoặc" />
      <TextField label="Email" value={email} onChangeText={setEmail} autoCapitalize="none" keyboardType="email-address" />
      <TextField label="Mật khẩu" value={password} onChangeText={setPassword} secureTextEntry />
      <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" style={{ alignSelf: 'flex-end' }}
        onPress={() => Alert.alert('Quên mật khẩu?', 'Tính năng đặt lại mật khẩu sắp ra mắt.')}>
        <AppText variant="captionStrong" color={colors.roseStrong}>Quên mật khẩu?</AppText>
      </Pressable>
      <ErrorText message={error} />
      <Button title="Đăng nhập" onPress={submit} loading={loading} />
    </Screen>
  );
}
```

```ts
import { countdownLabel, formatInviteCode, normalizeHandle } from '@/features/couple/handle';
import { greeting } from '@/lib/greeting';

test('greeting by hour', () => {
  expect(greeting(new Date(2026, 9, 11, 7))).toBe('Chào buổi sáng');
  expect(greeting(new Date(2026, 9, 11, 14))).toBe('Chào buổi chiều');
  expect(greeting(new Date(2026, 9, 11, 21))).toBe('Chào buổi tối');
  expect(greeting(new Date(2026, 9, 11, 2))).toBe('Chào buổi tối');
});

test('handle and invite helpers', () => {
  expect(normalizeHandle(' @Minh.Dev ')).toBe('minh.dev');
  expect(formatInviteCode('ABCD2345')).toBe('ABCD 2345');
  const now = new Date('2026-10-11T10:00:00Z');
  expect(countdownLabel('2026-10-11T10:59:12Z', now)).toBe('59:12');
  expect(countdownLabel('2026-10-11T09:00:00Z', now)).toBe('00:00');
});
```

```tsx
const NOT_PAIRED = { status: 409, body: { code: 'COUPLE_REQUIRED', detail: 'Bạn chưa ghép đôi' } };
const COUPLE = { id: 'c1', startedDatingOn: null, createdAt: '2026-10-11T00:00:00Z', partner: { id: 'u2', displayName: 'Minh', handle: 'minh.dev', birthday: null, avatarUrl: null } };
const INVITE = { status: 201, body: { code: 'ABCD2345', expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 3_550_000).toISOString() } };

describe('PairScreen', () => {
  test('invite tab creates a code right away', async () => {
    (useLocalSearchParams as jest.Mock).mockReturnValue({ tab: 'invite' });
    mockApi({ 'GET /api/couples/me': NOT_PAIRED, 'POST /api/couples/invites': INVITE });
    await renderWithProviders(<PairScreen />, { withSession: true });
    expect(await screen.findByText('ABCD 2345')).toBeOnTheScreen();
    expect(screen.getByText(/^Hết hạn sau \d\d:\d\d$/)).toBeOnTheScreen();
  });

  test('code tab joins with normalized code and chosen start date', async () => {
    (useLocalSearchParams as jest.Mock).mockReturnValue({ tab: 'code' });
    const api = mockApi({ 'GET /api/couples/me': NOT_PAIRED, 'POST /api/couples/join': { status: 201, body: COUPLE } });
    await renderWithProviders(<PairScreen />, { withSession: true });

    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByLabelText('Mã người ấy gửi bạn'), ' xy7k2m9p ');
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Ngày bắt đầu yêu' }));
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Ngày 1' }));
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Xác nhận' }));
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Kết nối' }));

    const firstOfMonth = `${toIsoDate(new Date()).slice(0, 8)}01`;
    expect(api.calls.find((c) => c.path === '/api/couples/join')?.body).toEqual({ code: 'XY7K2M9P', startedDatingOn: firstOfMonth });
    expect(router.replace).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/pair/success');
  });

  test('wrong code shows server message', async () => {
    (useLocalSearchParams as jest.Mock).mockReturnValue({ tab: 'code' });
    mockApi({
      'GET /api/couples/me': NOT_PAIRED,
      'POST /api/couples/join': { status: 400, body: { code: 'INVITE_INVALID', detail: 'Mã mời không đúng hoặc đã hết hạn' } },
    });
    await renderWithProviders(<PairScreen />, { withSession: true });
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByLabelText('Mã người ấy gửi bạn'), 'AAAA2222');
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Kết nối' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Mã mời không đúng hoặc đã hết hạn')).toBeOnTheScreen();
  });

  test('"Để sau" explains solo mode and enters the app', async () => {
    (useLocalSearchParams as jest.Mock).mockReturnValue({ tab: 'code' });
    mockApi({ 'GET /api/couples/me': NOT_PAIRED });
    await renderWithProviders(<PairScreen />, { withSession: true });
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Để sau' }));
    expect(screen.getByText('Ghép đôi sau cũng được')).toBeOnTheScreen();
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Vào app' }));
    expect(router.replace).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/');
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { fireEvent, screen } from '@testing-library/react-native';
import { router } from 'expo-router';

import PairSearchScreen from '@/features/couple/PairSearchScreen';
import { mockApi } from '@/test/api-mock';
import { renderWithProviders } from '@/test/render';

const ME = { id: 'u1', email: 'lan@x.vn', displayName: 'Lan', handle: 'lan.2001', birthday: null, timezone: 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh', avatarUrl: null };
const MINH = { id: 'u2', displayName: 'Minh', handle: 'minh.dev', avatarUrl: null };
const HOANG = { id: 'u3', displayName: 'Hoàng', handle: 'hoang.98', avatarUrl: null };
const COUPLE = { id: 'c1', startedDatingOn: null, createdAt: '2026-10-11T00:00:00Z', partner: { ...HOANG, birthday: null } };

function routes(extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  return {
    'GET /api/me': { body: ME },
    'GET /api/couples/requests': {
      body: { incoming: [{ id: 'r9', user: HOANG, createdAt: new Date().toISOString(), expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 86_000_000).toISOString() }], outgoing: [] },
    },
    ...extra,
  };
}

describe('PairSearchScreen', () => {
  test('shows my handle, finds a person and sends a request', async () => {
    const api = mockApi(routes({
      'GET /api/users/lookup': { body: MINH },
      'POST /api/couples/requests': { status: 201, body: { id: 'r1', user: MINH, createdAt: '', expiresAt: '' } },
    }));
    await renderWithProviders(<PairSearchScreen />);
    expect(await screen.findByText('@lan.2001')).toBeOnTheScreen();

    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByLabelText('Nhập ID người ấy'), '@minh.dev');
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Tìm' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Minh')).toBeOnTheScreen();
    expect(api.calls.find((c) => c.path.startsWith('/api/users/lookup'))?.path).toBe('/api/users/lookup?handle=minh.dev');

    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Gửi lời mời' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Đã gửi · chờ đồng ý')).toBeOnTheScreen();
    expect(api.calls.find((c) => c.method === 'POST')?.body).toEqual({ handle: 'minh.dev' });
  });

  test('unknown handle shows not found', async () => {
    mockApi(routes({ 'GET /api/users/lookup': { status: 404, body: { code: 'NOT_FOUND', detail: 'Không tìm thấy ID này' } } }));
    await renderWithProviders(<PairSearchScreen />);
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByLabelText('Nhập ID người ấy'), 'abc');
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Tìm' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Không tìm thấy ID này')).toBeOnTheScreen();
  });

  test('accepting an incoming request opens the success screen', async () => {
    mockApi(routes({ 'POST /api/couples/requests/r9/accept': { status: 201, body: COUPLE } }));
    await renderWithProviders(<PairSearchScreen />);
    expect(await screen.findByText('Hoàng muốn ghép đôi với bạn')).toBeOnTheScreen();
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Đồng ý' }));
    await screen.findByText('Hoàng muốn ghép đôi với bạn');
    expect(router.replace).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/pair/success');
  });
});
```

```ts
// src/lib/greeting.ts
export function greeting(now: Date): string {
  const h = now.getHours();
  if (h >= 5 && h < 11) return 'Chào buổi sáng';
  if (h >= 11 && h < 18) return 'Chào buổi chiều';
  return 'Chào buổi tối';
}
```

```ts
// src/features/couple/handle.ts
import { pad } from '@/lib/format';

export function normalizeHandle(raw: string): string {
  return raw.trim().replace(/^@/, '').toLowerCase();
}

export function formatInviteCode(code: string): string {
  return code.length === 8 ? `${code.slice(0, 4)} ${code.slice(4)}` : code;
}

/** Thời gian còn lại dạng mm:ss, không âm. */
export function countdownLabel(expiresAt: string, now: Date = new Date()): string {
  const s = Math.max(0, Math.floor((new Date(expiresAt).getTime() - now.getTime()) / 1000));
  return `${pad(Math.floor(s / 60))}:${pad(s % 60)}`;
}
```

```tsx
];
```

```tsx
import * as Clipboard from 'expo-clipboard';
import { router, useLocalSearchParams } from 'expo-router';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Pressable, Share, StyleSheet, TextInput, View } from 'react-native';

import { errorMessage } from '@/lib/api/client';
import type { Invite } from '@/lib/api/types';
import { formatDate, toIsoDate } from '@/lib/format';
import { AppText } from '@/ui/AppText';
import { Button } from '@/ui/Button';
import { Card } from '@/ui/Card';
import { DatePickerSheet } from '@/ui/DatePickerSheet';
import { ErrorText } from '@/ui/ErrorText';
import { Icon, IconName } from '@/ui/Icon';
import { IconCircle } from '@/ui/IconCircle';
import { Screen } from '@/ui/Screen';
import { SegmentedTabs } from '@/ui/SegmentedTabs';
import { Sheet } from '@/ui/Sheet';
import { colors, fonts } from '@/ui/theme';
import { TopBar } from '@/ui/TopBar';

import { useCouple, useCreateInvite, useJoinCouple } from './api';
import { countdownLabel, formatInviteCode } from './handle';

type Tab = 'invite' | 'code';

export default function PairScreen() {
  const params = useLocalSearchParams<{ tab?: Tab }>();
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>(params.tab === 'code' ? 'code' : 'invite');
  const [laterOpen, setLaterOpen] = useState(false);

  return (
    <Screen gap={16}>
      <TopBar right={
        <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Để sau" onPress={() => setLaterOpen(true)} hitSlop={8}>
          <AppText variant="bodyStrong" color={colors.roseStrong}>Để sau</AppText>
        </Pressable>
      } />
      <View style={{ gap: 6 }}>
        <AppText variant="display">Kết nối với người ấy</AppText>
        <AppText color={colors.textMuted}>Chọn một cách bên dưới.</AppText>
      </View>
      <SegmentedTabs options={[{ key: 'invite', label: 'Mời người ấy' }, { key: 'code', label: 'Nhập mã' }]} value={tab} onChange={setTab} />
      {tab === 'invite' ? <InvitePanel /> : <CodePanel />}
      <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" onPress={() => router.push('/pair/search')} style={styles.searchLink}>
        <Icon name="search" size={16} color={colors.roseStrong} />
        <AppText variant="captionStrong" color={colors.roseStrong}>Không có mã? Tìm theo ID</AppText>
      </Pressable>
      <LaterSheet visible={laterOpen} onClose={() => setLaterOpen(false)} />
    </Screen>
  );
}

function InvitePanel() {
  const create = useCreateInvite();
  const [invite, setInvite] = useState<Invite | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [now, setNow] = useState(new Date());
  const couple = useCouple(invite ? 5000 : undefined); // chờ người ấy nhập mã

  useEffect(() => {
    create.mutateAsync().then(setInvite, (e) => setError(errorMessage(e)));
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setInterval(() => setNow(new Date()), 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(t);
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (invite && couple.data) router.replace('/pair/success');
  }, [invite, couple.data]);

  return (
    <>
      <Card style={{ alignItems: 'center', gap: 14, borderRadius: 28, paddingTop: 22 }}>
        <AppText variant="micro" color={colors.textMuted}>MÃ MỜI CỦA BẠN</AppText>
        <View style={styles.codeBox}>
          <AppText selectable style={styles.code}>{invite ? formatInviteCode(invite.code) : '···· ····'}</AppText>
        </View>
        {invite ? (
          <View style={styles.expiry}>
            <Icon name="clock" size={16} color={colors.peachInk} />
            <AppText variant="captionStrong">Hết hạn sau {countdownLabel(invite.expiresAt, now)}</AppText>
          </View>
        ) : null}
        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 10, alignSelf: 'stretch' }}>
          <Button title="Sao chép" variant="secondary" compact style={{ flex: 1 }} disabled={!invite}
            onPress={() => invite && Clipboard.setStringAsync(invite.code)} />
          <Button title="Gửi người ấy" compact style={{ flex: 1 }} disabled={!invite}
            onPress={() => invite && Share.share({ message: `Mã ghép đôi Couple App của mình: ${invite.code}` })} />
        </View>
      </Card>
      <ErrorText message={error} />
      <View style={styles.how}>
        {['Gửi mã cho người ấy qua Zalo, Messenger…', 'Người ấy mở app, chọn tab “Nhập mã”', 'Hai bạn tự động được kết nối'].map((t, i) => (
          <View key={t} style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}>
            <View style={styles.step}><AppText variant="micro" color={colors.white}>{i + 1}</AppText></View>
            <AppText variant="caption">{t}</AppText>
          </View>
        ))}
      </View>
    </>
  );
}

function CodePanel() {
  const join = useJoinCouple();
  const [code, setCode] = useState('');
  const [startedOn, setStartedOn] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [pickerOpen, setPickerOpen] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function connect() {
    const normalized = code.replace(/\s/g, '').toUpperCase();
    if (!normalized) {
      setError('Nhập mã mời');
      return;
    }
    setError(null);
    try {
      await join.mutateAsync(startedOn ? { code: normalized, startedDatingOn: startedOn } : { code: normalized });
      router.replace('/pair/success');
    } catch (e) {
      setError(errorMessage(e));
    }
  }

  return (
    <>
      <Card style={{ borderRadius: 28, gap: 14 }}>
        <AppText variant="h3">Mã người ấy gửi bạn</AppText>
        <TextInput
          accessibilityLabel="Mã người ấy gửi bạn"
          value={code}
          onChangeText={setCode}
          autoCapitalize="characters"
          maxLength={9}
          placeholder="XY7K 2M9P"
          placeholderTextColor={colors.roseBorder}
          style={styles.codeInput}
        />
        <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Ngày bắt đầu yêu" onPress={() => setPickerOpen(true)} style={styles.dateRow}>
          <IconCircle name="calendar" bg={colors.surface} color={colors.rose} size={36} />
          <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
            <AppText variant="bodyStrong">Ngày bắt đầu yêu</AppText>
            <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted}>{startedOn ? formatDate(startedOn) : 'Chọn ngày (không bắt buộc)'}</AppText>
          </View>
          <Icon name="chevronRight" size={18} color={colors.textMuted} />
        </Pressable>
        <ErrorText message={error} />
        <Button title="Kết nối" onPress={connect} loading={join.isPending} />
      </Card>
      <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted} style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>
        Mã gồm 8 ký tự, hết hạn sau 1 giờ kể từ khi người ấy tạo.
      </AppText>
      <DatePickerSheet
        visible={pickerOpen}
        title="Ngày hai bạn bắt đầu yêu"
        value={startedOn}
        maxDate={toIsoDate(new Date())}
        onClose={() => setPickerOpen(false)}
        onConfirm={(d) => {
          setStartedOn(d);
          setPickerOpen(false);
        }}
      />
    </>
  );
}

const SOLO_FEATURES: { icon: IconName; bg: string; color: string; label: string }[] = [
  { icon: 'image', bg: colors.lavender, color: colors.lavenderInk, label: 'Nhật ký & khoảnh khắc của riêng bạn' },
  { icon: 'droplet', bg: colors.roseSoft, color: colors.cycle, label: 'Theo dõi chu kỳ (tuỳ chọn)' },
  { icon: 'gift', bg: colors.peachSoft, color: colors.peachInk, label: 'Nhắc sinh nhật, ngày quan trọng' },
];

function LaterSheet({ visible, onClose }: { visible: boolean; onClose: () => void }) {
  return (
    <Sheet visible={visible} onClose={onClose}>
      <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', gap: 14 }}>
        <IconCircle name="heart" bg={colors.roseSoft} color={colors.rose} size={64} />
        <AppText variant="h2" style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>Ghép đôi sau cũng được</AppText>
        <AppText color={colors.textMuted} style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>Chưa có người ấy hay vừa kết thúc? Bạn vẫn dùng được:</AppText>
      </View>
      <Card>
        {SOLO_FEATURES.map((f) => (
          <View key={f.label} style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12 }}>
            <IconCircle name={f.icon} bg={f.bg} color={f.color} size={32} />
            <AppText variant="bodyStrong" style={{ flex: 1 }}>{f.label}</AppText>
          </View>
        ))}
      </Card>
      <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted} style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>
        Ghép đôi lúc nào cũng được ở Trang chủ hoặc Tôi. Kỷ niệm riêng vẫn là của bạn — khi ghép đôi, bạn tự chọn có chia sẻ hay không.
      </AppText>
      <Button title="Vào app" onPress={() => { onClose(); router.replace('/'); }} />
      <Button title="Quay lại ghép đôi" variant="ghost" onPress={onClose} />
    </Sheet>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  codeBox: { alignSelf: 'stretch', paddingVertical: 16, borderRadius: 20, borderWidth: 1.5, borderStyle: 'dashed', borderColor: colors.rose, backgroundColor: colors.roseSoft, alignItems: 'center' },
  code: { fontFamily: fonts.extrabold, fontSize: 36, letterSpacing: 8, color: colors.roseStrong },
  expiry: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, paddingVertical: 8, paddingHorizontal: 14, borderRadius: 999, backgroundColor: colors.peachSoft },
  how: { padding: 16, borderRadius: 20, backgroundColor: colors.surfaceMuted, gap: 10 },
  step: { width: 24, height: 24, borderRadius: 12, backgroundColor: colors.roseStrong, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  codeInput: { height: 56, borderRadius: 16, backgroundColor: colors.roseSoft, textAlign: 'center', fontFamily: fonts.bold, fontSize: 24, letterSpacing: 6, color: colors.roseStrong },
  dateRow: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, padding: 14, borderRadius: 18, backgroundColor: colors.surfaceMuted },
  searchLink: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 6, alignSelf: 'center', height: 44, paddingHorizontal: 16, borderRadius: 999, borderWidth: 1.5, borderColor: colors.line, backgroundColor: colors.surface },
});
```

```tsx
import { router } from 'expo-router';
import { LinearGradient } from 'expo-linear-gradient';
import { View } from 'react-native';

import { useMe } from '@/features/account/api';
import { daysBetween, formatDate } from '@/lib/format';
import { AppText } from '@/ui/AppText';
import { Avatar } from '@/ui/Avatar';
import { Button } from '@/ui/Button';
import { Card } from '@/ui/Card';
import { Icon } from '@/ui/Icon';
import { colors } from '@/ui/theme';

import { useCouple } from './api';

export default function PairSuccessScreen() {
  const couple = useCouple();
  const me = useMe();
  const c = couple.data;
  const since = c?.startedDatingOn ?? c?.createdAt.slice(0, 10) ?? null;
  const days = since ? Math.max(0, daysBetween(since, new Date())) : 0;
  return (
    <LinearGradient colors={['#FDE3EA', colors.bg]} locations={[0, 0.7]} style={{ flex: 1, paddingHorizontal: 24, justifyContent: 'center', gap: 12 }}>
      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignSelf: 'center', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 24 }}>
        <Avatar name={me.data?.displayName ?? 'B'} tone="rose" size={84} ring uri={me.data?.avatarUrl} />
        <View style={{ marginLeft: -6 }}><Avatar name={c?.partner.displayName ?? '?'} tone="lavender" size={84} ring uri={c?.partner.avatarUrl} /></View>
        <View style={{ position: 'absolute', left: 57, top: 36, width: 56, height: 56, borderRadius: 28, borderWidth: 4, borderColor: colors.white, backgroundColor: colors.rose, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
          <Icon name="heartFilled" size={22} color={colors.white} />
        </View>
      </View>
      <AppText variant="display" style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>Hai bạn đã được kết nối!</AppText>
      <AppText color={colors.textMuted} style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>
        {me.data?.displayName} & {c?.partner.displayName}{since ? ` · bên nhau từ ${formatDate(since)}` : ''}
      </AppText>
      <Card style={{ alignItems: 'center', gap: 0, borderRadius: 28, marginTop: 8 }}>
        <AppText style={{ fontFamily: 'BeVietnamPro_800ExtraBold', fontSize: 56, lineHeight: 64 }} color={colors.roseStrong}>{days}</AppText>
        <AppText variant="h3" color={colors.textMuted}>ngày bên nhau</AppText>
      </Card>
      <View style={{ height: 24 }} />
      <Button title="Vào trang chủ" onPress={() => router.replace('/')} />
      <Button title="Thêm kỷ niệm đầu tiên" variant="ghost" onPress={() => router.replace('/create')} />
    </LinearGradient>
  );
}
```
