- [ ] **Step 4: `moment-input.ts` & `upload-capture.ts`**

```ts
// src/features/create/moment-input.ts
import { moodLabel } from '@/features/moments/labels';

/** Tiêu đề bắt buộc ở backend: dòng đầu của lời viết, nếu trống thì theo cảm xúc. */
export function momentTitle(caption: string, mood: string | null): string {
  const first = caption.trim().split('\n')[0].trim();
  if (first) return first.slice(0, 60);
  const label = moodLabel(mood);
  return label && mood !== 'OTHER' ? `Khoảnh khắc ${label.toLowerCase()}` : 'Khoảnh khắc';
}
```

```ts
// src/features/create/upload-capture.ts
import { uploadImage } from '@/features/media/upload';

import type { Capture } from './capture-store';
import { renderComposition } from './render-effects';

/** Áp hiệu ứng rồi tải lần lượt từng ảnh; một ảnh lỗi → cả lô lỗi để không tạo kỷ niệm thiếu ảnh. */
export async function uploadCapture(capture: Capture): Promise<string[]> {
  const ids: string[] = [];
  for (const uri of capture.uris) {
    const rendered = await renderComposition(uri, capture);
    const media = await uploadImage(rendered, { purpose: 'SHARED', filter: capture.effect });
    ids.push(media.id);
  }
  return ids;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: `PlaceSheet.tsx`**

```tsx
import * as Location from 'expo-location';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

import type { PlaceInput } from '@/lib/api/types';
import { AppText } from '@/ui/AppText';
import { Button } from '@/ui/Button';
import { ErrorText } from '@/ui/ErrorText';
import { Sheet } from '@/ui/Sheet';
import { TextField } from '@/ui/TextField';
import { colors } from '@/ui/theme';

type Props = { visible: boolean; value: PlaceInput | null; onChange: (p: PlaceInput | null) => void; onClose: () => void };

export function PlaceSheet({ visible, value, onChange, onClose }: Props) {
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [coords, setCoords] = useState<{ lat: number; lng: number } | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [locating, setLocating] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!visible) return;
    setName(value?.name ?? '');
    setCoords(value ? { lat: value.lat, lng: value.lng } : null);
    setError(null);
  }, [visible, value]);

  async function locate() {
    setLocating(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const p = await Location.requestForegroundPermissionsAsync();
      if (!p.granted) {
        setError('Cần cho phép truy cập vị trí');
        return;
      }
      const pos = await Location.getCurrentPositionAsync({ accuracy: Location.Accuracy.Balanced });
      setCoords({ lat: pos.coords.latitude, lng: pos.coords.longitude });
    } catch {
      setError('Không lấy được vị trí, thử lại nhé');
    } finally {
      setLocating(false);
    }
  }

  function save() {
    if (!name.trim()) return setError('Đặt tên cho địa điểm');
    if (!coords) return setError('Bấm "Dùng vị trí hiện tại" để lấy toạ độ');
    onChange({ name: name.trim(), lat: coords.lat, lng: coords.lng });
    onClose();
  }

  return (
    <Sheet visible={visible} onClose={onClose} title="Địa điểm" subtitle="Hiện trên bản đồ hẹn hò sau này">
      <TextField label="Tên địa điểm" value={name} onChangeText={setName} placeholder="VD: Cộng Cà Phê Hoàn Kiếm" />
      <Button title={coords ? 'Đã lấy vị trí hiện tại' : 'Dùng vị trí hiện tại'} icon="navigation" variant="secondary" onPress={locate} loading={locating} />
      {coords ? <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted}>{coords.lat.toFixed(4)}, {coords.lng.toFixed(4)}</AppText> : null}
      <ErrorText message={error} />
      <Button title="Xong" onPress={save} />
      {value ? <Button title="Bỏ địa điểm" variant="ghost" onPress={() => { onChange(null); onClose(); }} /> : null}
    </Sheet>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: `MomentCaptureScreen.tsx` (C9)**

```tsx
import * as ImagePicker from 'expo-image-picker';
import { router } from 'expo-router';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Pressable, ScrollView, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';

import { useCouple } from '@/features/couple/api';
import { useCreateMoment } from '@/features/moments/api';
import { MOODS, moodLabel } from '@/features/moments/labels';
import { PlaceSheet } from '@/features/moments/PlaceSheet';
import { errorMessage } from '@/lib/api/client';
import type { MomentInput, PlaceInput } from '@/lib/api/types';
import { combineDateTime, formatDate, pad, toIsoDate } from '@/lib/format';
import { AppText } from '@/ui/AppText';
import { Button } from '@/ui/Button';
import { Card } from '@/ui/Card';
import { Chip } from '@/ui/Chip';
import { DatePickerSheet } from '@/ui/DatePickerSheet';
import { ErrorText } from '@/ui/ErrorText';
import { Icon } from '@/ui/Icon';
import { ListRow } from '@/ui/ListRow';
import { PhotoTile } from '@/ui/PhotoTile';
import { Screen } from '@/ui/Screen';
import { TextField } from '@/ui/TextField';
import { colors } from '@/ui/theme';
import { Toggle } from '@/ui/Toggle';
import { TopBar } from '@/ui/TopBar';

import { captureStore, useCapture } from './capture-store';
import { momentTitle } from './moment-input';
import { uploadCapture } from './upload-capture';

export default function MomentCaptureScreen() {
  const capture = useCapture();
  const couple = useCouple();
  const create = useCreateMoment();
  const partner = couple.data?.partner;
  const now = new Date();
  const [caption, setCaption] = useState(capture.caption);
  const [mood, setMood] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [date, setDate] = useState(capture.date || toIsoDate(now));
  const [time, setTime] = useState(`${pad(now.getHours())}:${pad(now.getMinutes())}`);
  const [place, setPlace] = useState<PlaceInput | null>(null);
  const [notify, setNotify] = useState(true);
  const [timeOpen, setTimeOpen] = useState(false);
  const [placeOpen, setPlaceOpen] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function addPhotos() {
    const res = await ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync({ mediaTypes: ['images'], allowsMultipleSelection: true, selectionLimit: 10, quality: 0.9 });
    if (!res.canceled && res.assets?.length) captureStore.set({ uris: [...capture.uris, ...res.assets.map((a) => a.uri)].slice(0, 30) });
  }

  async function save() {
    if (capture.uris.length === 0 && !caption.trim()) {
      setError('Thêm ảnh hoặc viết vài dòng nhé');
      return;
    }
    setSaving(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const mediaIds = await uploadCapture(capture);
      const input: MomentInput = {
        type: 'MOMENT',
        title: momentTitle(caption, mood),
        occursOn: date,
        occursAt: combineDateTime(date, time),
        message: caption.trim(),
        details: mood ? { mood } : {},
        mediaIds,
      };
      if (partner) {
        input.notifyPartner = notify;
        if (place) input.place = place;
      }
      await create.mutateAsync(input);
      captureStore.reset();
      router.replace(`/journal/${date}`);
    } catch (e) {
      setError(errorMessage(e));
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  const label = moodLabel(mood);
  const preview = `${partner?.displayName} nhận: “Bạn vừa lưu một khoảnh khắc ${label && mood !== 'OTHER' ? label.toLowerCase() : 'mới'}”`;

  return (
    <Screen bottomPad={40}>
      <TopBar title="Khoảnh khắc" right={<View style={styles.badge}><AppText variant="micro" color={colors.mintInk}>Không nhắc nhở</AppText></View>} />
      <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={{ gap: 10 }}>
        {capture.uris.map((uri, i) => (
          <PhotoTile key={uri} uri={uri} seed={uri} style={styles.thumb}>
            <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel={`Bỏ ảnh ${i + 1}`} style={styles.remove}
              onPress={() => captureStore.set({ uris: capture.uris.filter((u) => u !== uri) })}>
              <Icon name="close" size={12} color={colors.white} />
            </Pressable>
          </PhotoTile>
        ))}
        <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Thêm ảnh" onPress={addPhotos} style={styles.add}>
          <Icon name="plus" size={20} color={colors.roseStrong} />
          <AppText variant="captionStrong" color={colors.roseStrong}>Thêm</AppText>
        </Pressable>
      </ScrollView>
      <TextField label="Bạn đang nghĩ gì?" value={caption} onChangeText={setCaption} multiline placeholder="Viết vài dòng cho khoảnh khắc này…" />
      <AppText variant="micro" color={colors.textMuted}>CẢM XÚC</AppText>
      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 8 }}>
        {MOODS.map((m) => (
          <Chip key={m.code} label={m.code === 'OTHER' ? '+ Khác' : m.label} selected={mood === m.code} onPress={() => setMood(mood === m.code ? null : m.code)} />
        ))}
      </View>
      <Card style={{ paddingVertical: 4, gap: 0 }}>
        <ListRow icon="clock" iconBg={colors.surfaceMuted} iconColor={colors.textMuted} title="Thời gian"
          right={<AppText variant="bodyStrong">{date === toIsoDate(now) ? 'Hôm nay' : formatDate(date)}, {time}</AppText>}
          onPress={() => setTimeOpen(true)} />
        {partner ? (
          <ListRow icon="pin" iconBg={colors.surfaceMuted} iconColor={colors.textMuted} title="Địa điểm"
            right={<AppText variant="bodyStrong" numberOfLines={1} style={{ maxWidth: 160 }}>{place?.name ?? 'Thêm'}</AppText>}
            onPress={() => setPlaceOpen(true)} />
        ) : null}
      </Card>
      {partner ? (
        <Card style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12 }}>
          <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
            <AppText variant="bodyStrong">Báo cho {partner.displayName} biết</AppText>
            <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted}>{notify ? preview : 'Chỉ lưu vào Nhật ký, không gửi thông báo'}</AppText>
          </View>
          <Toggle label={`Báo cho ${partner.displayName} biết`} value={notify} onValueChange={setNotify} />
        </Card>
      ) : (
        <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted}>Khoảnh khắc này chỉ mình bạn xem được.</AppText>
      )}
      <ErrorText message={error} />
      <Button title="Lưu vào Nhật ký" onPress={save} loading={saving} />

      <DatePickerSheet visible={timeOpen} mode="datetime" title="Thời gian" value={date} time={time} maxDate={toIsoDate(now)}
        onClose={() => setTimeOpen(false)} onConfirm={(d, t) => { setDate(d); if (t) setTime(t); setTimeOpen(false); }} />
      <PlaceSheet visible={placeOpen} value={place} onChange={setPlace} onClose={() => setPlaceOpen(false)} />
    </Screen>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  badge: { paddingHorizontal: 10, paddingVertical: 5, borderRadius: 999, backgroundColor: colors.mint },
  thumb: { width: 76, height: 96, borderRadius: 18 },
  remove: { position: 'absolute', right: 6, top: 6, width: 22, height: 22, borderRadius: 11, backgroundColor: 'rgba(43,26,46,0.6)', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  add: { width: 76, height: 96, borderRadius: 18, borderWidth: 1.5, borderStyle: 'dashed', borderColor: colors.rose, backgroundColor: colors.roseSoft, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 4 },
});
```

```ts
import type { Moment, MomentInput, MomentType, PlaceInput, Recurrence } from '@/lib/api/types';

export type MomentForm = {
  type: MomentType;
  title: string;
  date: string;
  time: string | null;
  message: string;
  occasion: string | null;
  subject: SubjectChoice | null;
  recurrence: Recurrence;
  place: PlaceInput | null;
};

export function repeatOptions(type: MomentType): Recurrence[] {
  return type === 'CUSTOM' || type === 'DATE_PLAN' ? ['NONE', 'WEEKLY', 'MONTHLY', 'YEARLY'] : [];
}

export function emptyForm(today: Date, type: MomentType = 'ANNIVERSARY'): MomentForm {
  return {
    type,
    title: '',
    date: toIsoDate(today),
    time: type === 'DATE_PLAN' ? '19:00' : null,
    message: '',
    occasion: null,
    subject: null,
    recurrence: type === 'GIFT_OCCASION' ? 'YEARLY' : 'NONE',
    place: null,
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `occurrence.ts`**

Thay `nextOccurrence` và thêm 2 hàm:

```ts
import type { Moment, Recurrence } from '@/lib/api/types';
import { weekdayIndex, weekdayName } from '@/lib/calendar';
import { daysBetween, parseIsoDate, toIsoDate } from '@/lib/format';

function safeDate(year: number, month: number, day: number): Date {
  const lastDay = new Date(year, month, 0).getDate();
  return new Date(year, month - 1, Math.min(day, lastDay));
}

export function nextOccurrence(m: Moment, today: Date): string | null {
  const todayIso = toIsoDate(today);
  if (m.occursOn >= todayIso) return m.occursOn;
  const [y, month, day] = m.occursOn.split('-').map(Number);
  if (m.recurrence === 'WEEKLY') {
    const gap = daysBetween(m.occursOn, today);
    const start = parseIsoDate(m.occursOn);
    start.setDate(start.getDate() + Math.ceil(gap / 7) * 7);
    return toIsoDate(start);
  }
  if (m.recurrence === 'MONTHLY') {
    for (let k = (today.getFullYear() - y) * 12 + (today.getMonth() + 1 - month); k < 1200; k++) {
      const candidate = toIsoDate(safeDate(y, month + k, day));
      if (candidate >= todayIso) return candidate;
    }
    return null;
  }
  if (m.recurrence === 'YEARLY' || m.type === 'ANNIVERSARY') {
    for (const year of [today.getFullYear(), today.getFullYear() + 1]) {
      const candidate = toIsoDate(safeDate(year, month, day));
      if (candidate >= todayIso) return candidate;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

export function recurrenceSummary(r: Recurrence, dateIso: string): string {
  const [, mm, dd] = dateIso.split('-');
  switch (r) {
    case 'WEEKLY':
      return `Mỗi ${weekdayName(dateIso)}`;
    case 'MONTHLY':
      return `Ngày ${Number(dd)} mỗi tháng`;
    case 'YEARLY':
      return `${dd}/${mm} mỗi năm`;
    default:
      return 'Chỉ nhắc một lần';
  }
}

/** Ngày gần nhất từ `dateIso` trở đi rơi vào `weekday` (0 = Thứ Hai). */
export function alignToWeekday(dateIso: string, weekday: number): string {
  const d = parseIsoDate(dateIso);
  d.setDate(d.getDate() + ((weekday - weekdayIndex(dateIso) + 7) % 7));
  return toIsoDate(d);
}
```

(`safeDate(y, month + k, day)` dùng `new Date(year, month, 0)` nên tháng > 12 tự cuộn năm.) Giữ nguyên `upcomingMoments`.

- [ ] **Step 6: `RepeatSheet.tsx` (C5)**

```tsx
import { Pressable, View } from 'react-native';

import type { Recurrence } from '@/lib/api/types';
import { WEEKDAYS, weekdayIndex } from '@/lib/calendar';
import { AppText } from '@/ui/AppText';
import { Button } from '@/ui/Button';
import { Icon } from '@/ui/Icon';
import { Sheet } from '@/ui/Sheet';
import { colors } from '@/ui/theme';

import { alignToWeekday, recurrenceSummary } from './occurrence';

const TITLES: Record<Recurrence, string> = { NONE: 'Không lặp lại', WEEKLY: 'Hằng tuần', MONTHLY: 'Hằng tháng', YEARLY: 'Hằng năm' };

type Props = {
  visible: boolean;
  value: Recurrence;
  date: string;
  options: Recurrence[];
  onChange: (r: Recurrence, date: string) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
};

export function RepeatSheet({ visible, value, date, options, onChange, onClose }: Props) {
  const weekday = weekdayIndex(date);
  return (
    <Sheet visible={visible} onClose={onClose} title="Lặp lại">
      <View accessibilityRole="radiogroup" style={{ gap: 10 }}>
        {options.map((r) => {
          const on = r === value;
          return (
            <Pressable key={r} accessibilityRole="radio" accessibilityLabel={TITLES[r]} accessibilityState={{ checked: on }}
              onPress={() => onChange(r, date)}
              style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, padding: 14, borderRadius: 18, borderWidth: 1.5, borderColor: on ? colors.rose : 'transparent', backgroundColor: on ? colors.roseSoft : colors.surface }}>
              <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                <AppText variant="bodyStrong">{TITLES[r]}</AppText>
                <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted}>{recurrenceSummary(r, date)}</AppText>
              </View>
              <View style={{ width: 22, height: 22, borderRadius: 11, borderWidth: 2, borderColor: on ? colors.roseStrong : colors.toggleOff, backgroundColor: on ? colors.roseStrong : 'transparent', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
                {on ? <Icon name="check" size={14} color={colors.white} /> : null}
              </View>
            </Pressable>
          );
        })}
      </View>
      {value === 'WEEKLY' ? (
        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 6 }}>
          {WEEKDAYS.map((w, i) => (
            <Pressable key={w} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel={`Lặp vào ${w}`} accessibilityState={{ selected: i === weekday }}
              onPress={() => onChange('WEEKLY', alignToWeekday(date, i))}
              style={{ flex: 1, height: 38, borderRadius: 12, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', backgroundColor: i === weekday ? colors.roseStrong : colors.surface }}>
              <AppText variant="captionStrong" color={i === weekday ? colors.white : colors.text}>{w}</AppText>
            </Pressable>
          ))}
        </View>
      ) : null}
      <Button title="Xong" onPress={onClose} />
    </Sheet>
  );
}
```

> Chọn thứ khác sẽ dời ngày của kỷ niệm sang thứ đó (gần nhất từ ngày đang chọn) vì backend lặp theo thứ của `occursOn` (spec §12.3).

```tsx
const COUPLE = { id: 'c1', startedDatingOn: null, createdAt: '', partner: { id: 'u2', displayName: 'Minh', handle: 'minh', birthday: null, avatarUrl: null } };

    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Lưu' }));

    await screen.findByLabelText('Tiêu đề');
    expect(api.calls.find((c) => c.method === 'POST')?.body).toMatchObject({
      type: 'CUSTOM', title: 'Gọi video tối thứ Sáu', occursOn: '2026-02-13', recurrence: 'WEEKLY', mediaIds: ['p1'],

    await renderWithProviders(<MilestoneFormScreen mode="create" />);
    await fireEvent.changeText(await screen.findByLabelText('Tiêu đề'), 'Ăn tối');
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Ngày' }));
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByLabelText('Giờ'), '20');
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Xác nhận' }));
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Lưu' }));
    await screen.findByLabelText('Tiêu đề');
    const body = api.calls.find((c) => c.method === 'POST')?.body as { occursAt: string };
    expect(new Date(body.occursAt).getHours()).toBe(20);
  });
});
```

```tsx
  subjectUserId: null, message: 'Lần đầu tặng socola tự làm', details: {}, reminderConfig: null, effectiveReminderConfig: CFG,
  place: { id: 'pl', name: 'Nhà hàng Ý', address: null, lat: 1, lng: 2, category: null }, media: [], createdBy: 'u1',

    expect(screen.getByText('Lần đầu tặng socola tự làm')).toBeOnTheScreen();
    expect(await screen.findByText('Còn 3 ngày nữa: Valentine đầu tiên')).toBeOnTheScreen();
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Tuỳ chỉnh' }));
    expect(router.push).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/moments/m1/reminders');
  });

  test('a moment (Khoảnh khắc) shows its mood and no reminder card', async () => {
    mockApi({
      'GET /api/me': { body: ME },
      'GET /api/couples/me': { body: COUPLE },
      'GET /api/moments/m1': { body: { ...VALENTINE, type: 'MOMENT', recurrence: 'NONE', details: { mood: 'MISSING' }, place: null } },
      'GET /api/moments/m1/reminders': { body: [] },
    });
    await renderWithProviders(<MomentDetailScreen />);
    expect(await screen.findByText('Nhớ nhung')).toBeOnTheScreen();
    expect(screen.queryByText('Nhắc nhở')).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

`src/features/reminders/__tests__/reminder-screen-test.tsx` (thay toàn bộ):

```tsx
import { fireEvent, screen } from '@testing-library/react-native';
import { router, useLocalSearchParams } from 'expo-router';

import ReminderConfigScreen from '@/features/reminders/ReminderConfigScreen';
import { mockApi } from '@/test/api-mock';
import { renderWithProviders } from '@/test/render';

const ME = { id: 'u1', email: 'lan@x.vn', displayName: 'Lan', handle: 'lan', birthday: null, timezone: 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh', avatarUrl: null };
const COUPLE = { id: 'c1', startedDatingOn: null, createdAt: '', partner: { id: 'u2', displayName: 'Minh', handle: 'minh', birthday: null, avatarUrl: null } };
const MOMENT = {
  id: 'm1', type: 'CUSTOM', title: 'Valentine', occursOn: '2026-02-14', occursAt: null, recurrence: 'YEARLY', subjectUserId: null,
  message: null, details: {}, reminderConfig: null,
  effectiveReminderConfig: { milestoneDays: null, yearly: null, offsets: ['P0D', 'P3D'], sendAt: '08:00', recipients: 'BOTH' },
  place: null, media: [], createdBy: 'u1', createdAt: '', updatedAt: '', personal: false,
};

function routes(extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  return {
    'GET /api/me': { body: ME },
    'GET /api/couples/me': { body: COUPLE },
    'GET /api/moments/m1': { body: MOMENT },
    'GET /api/moments/m1/reminders': { body: [] },
    'PUT /api/moments/m1/reminder-config': { body: MOMENT },
    ...extra,
  };
}

describe('ReminderConfigScreen', () => {
  beforeEach(() => (useLocalSearchParams as jest.Mock).mockReturnValue({ id: 'm1' }));

  test('changes recipients, offsets and send time then saves', async () => {
    const api = mockApi(routes());
    await renderWithProviders(<ReminderConfigScreen />);
    expect(await screen.findByText('2/5 đã chọn')).toBeOnTheScreen();
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: 'Người ấy' }));
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Trước 7 ngày' }));
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Giờ gửi' }));
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByLabelText('Giờ'), '20');
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Xác nhận' }));
    expect(screen.getByText('Còn 7 ngày nữa là Valentine · Gửi cho Minh')).toBeOnTheScreen();
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Lưu' }));

    await screen.findByText('3/5 đã chọn');
    expect(api.calls.find((c) => c.method === 'PUT')?.body).toEqual({
      recipients: 'PARTNER', offsets: ['P7D', 'P3D', 'P0D'], sendAt: '20:00', yearly: null, milestoneDays: null,
    });
    expect(router.back).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  test('restore defaults sends null', async () => {
    const api = mockApi(routes());
    await renderWithProviders(<ReminderConfigScreen />);
    await fireEvent.press(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Khôi phục mặc định' }));
    await screen.findByText('2/5 đã chọn');
    expect(api.calls.find((c) => c.method === 'PUT')?.body).toBeUndefined(); // body null → không gửi JSON
  });

  test('personal moments only remind the owner', async () => {
    mockApi(routes({ 'GET /api/moments/m1': { body: { ...MOMENT, personal: true } } }));
    await renderWithProviders(<ReminderConfigScreen />);
    expect(await screen.findByText('Kỷ niệm riêng — chỉ nhắc cho bạn')).toBeOnTheScreen();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('radio', { name: 'Cả hai' })).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

> Ở test "restore", `apiFetch` với `body: null` — kiểm tra `client.ts`: nếu `body === null` vẫn gửi chuỗi `"null"` thì `api-mock` parse ra `null`; đổi kỳ vọng thành `toBeNull()` cho khớp hành vi thật (backend cần body `null`).

```tsx
import * as ImagePicker from 'expo-image-picker';
import { router, useLocalSearchParams } from 'expo-router';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Alert, Pressable, ScrollView, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';

import { useMe } from '@/features/account/api';
import { useCouple } from '@/features/couple/api';
import { captureStore, useCapture } from '@/features/create/capture-store';
import { uploadCapture } from '@/features/create/upload-capture';
import { uploadImage } from '@/features/media/upload';
import { errorMessage } from '@/lib/api/client';
import type { MomentType } from '@/lib/api/types';
import { formatDate, toIsoDate } from '@/lib/format';
import { AppText } from '@/ui/AppText';
import { Button } from '@/ui/Button';
import { Card } from '@/ui/Card';
import { Chip } from '@/ui/Chip';
import { DatePickerSheet } from '@/ui/DatePickerSheet';
import { ErrorText } from '@/ui/ErrorText';
import { Icon } from '@/ui/Icon';
import { ListRow } from '@/ui/ListRow';
import { PhotoTile } from '@/ui/PhotoTile';
import { Screen } from '@/ui/Screen';
import { TextField } from '@/ui/TextField';
import { colors } from '@/ui/theme';
import { TopBar } from '@/ui/TopBar';

import { useCreateMoment, useDeleteMoment, useMoment, useUpdateMoment } from './api';
import { applyOccasion, emptyForm, formFromMoment, MomentForm, repeatOptions, toMomentInput, validateForm } from './form';
import { OCCASIONS, TYPE_LABELS } from './labels';
import { recurrenceSummary } from './occurrence';
import { PlaceSheet } from './PlaceSheet';
import { RepeatSheet } from './RepeatSheet';

const TYPES: MomentType[] = ['ANNIVERSARY', 'DATE_PLAN', 'GIFT_OCCASION', 'CUSTOM'];
const NEW_TITLES: Partial<Record<MomentType, string>> = { ANNIVERSARY: 'Kỷ niệm mới', DATE_PLAN: 'Lịch hẹn mới', GIFT_OCCASION: 'Dịp tặng quà mới', CUSTOM: 'Mốc mới' };

export default function MilestoneFormScreen({ mode }: { mode: 'create' | 'edit' }) {
  const params = useLocalSearchParams<{ type?: MomentType; id?: string }>();
  const me = useMe();
  const couple = useCouple();
  const capture = useCapture();
  const existing = useMoment(mode === 'edit' ? params.id ?? '' : '');
  const create = useCreateMoment();
  const update = useUpdateMoment(params.id ?? '');
  const remove = useDeleteMoment();
  const partner = couple.data?.partner;
  const [form, setForm] = useState<MomentForm>(() => {
    const f = emptyForm(new Date(), params.type ?? 'ANNIVERSARY');
    return capture.date ? { ...f, date: capture.date } : f;
  });
  const [newPhotos, setNewPhotos] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(mode === 'create');
  const [sheet, setSheet] = useState<'date' | 'repeat' | 'place' | null>(null);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (mode === 'edit' && existing.data && me.data && !loaded) {
      setForm(formFromMoment(existing.data, me.data.id));
      setLoaded(true);
    }
  }, [mode, existing.data, me.data, loaded]);

  const set = (patch: Partial<MomentForm>) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, ...patch }));
  const photos = mode === 'create' ? capture.uris : [...(existing.data?.media.map((m) => m.url) ?? []), ...newPhotos];

  async function addPhotos() {
    const res = await ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync({ mediaTypes: ['images'], allowsMultipleSelection: true, selectionLimit: 10, quality: 0.9 });
    if (res.canceled || !res.assets?.length) return;
    const uris = res.assets.map((a) => a.uri);
    if (mode === 'create') captureStore.set({ uris: [...capture.uris, ...uris] });
    else setNewPhotos([...newPhotos, ...uris]);
  }

  async function save() {
    const problem = validateForm(form);
    if (problem) return setError(problem);
    if (!me.data) return;
    setSaving(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const ids = { me: me.data.id, partner: partner?.id ?? me.data.id };
      if (mode === 'create') {
        const mediaIds = await uploadCapture(capture);
        const created = await create.mutateAsync(toMomentInput(form, ids, mediaIds, 'create'));
        captureStore.reset();
        router.replace(`/moments/${created.id}`);
      } else {
        const added: string[] = [];
        for (const uri of newPhotos) added.push((await uploadImage(uri, { purpose: 'SHARED' })).id);
        const mediaIds = [...(existing.data?.media.map((m) => m.id) ?? []), ...added];
        await update.mutateAsync(toMomentInput(form, ids, mediaIds, 'edit'));
        router.back();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      setError(errorMessage(e));
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  function confirmDelete() {
    Alert.alert('Xoá kỷ niệm?', 'Ảnh và nhắc nhở của kỷ niệm này cũng bị xoá.', [
      { text: 'Huỷ', style: 'cancel' },
      { text: 'Xoá', style: 'destructive', onPress: async () => { await remove.mutateAsync(params.id!); router.replace('/journal'); } },
    ]);
  }

  if (!loaded) return <Screen><AppText color={colors.textMuted}>Đang tải…</AppText></Screen>;

  const options = repeatOptions(form.type);
  const repeatSubtitle = form.type === 'ANNIVERSARY' ? 'Theo mốc ngày yêu · 100, 200… và mỗi năm'
    : form.type === 'GIFT_OCCASION' ? recurrenceSummary('YEARLY', form.date) : recurrenceSummary(form.recurrence, form.date);
  const dateLabel = `${formatDate(form.date)}${form.time ? ` · ${form.time}` : ''}`;

  return (
    <Screen bottomPad={40}>
      <TopBar backIcon="close" title={mode === 'edit' ? 'Sửa kỷ niệm' : NEW_TITLES[form.type] ?? 'Kỷ niệm mới'}
        right={<Pressable accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Lưu" onPress={save} disabled={saving} hitSlop={8}>
          <AppText variant="bodyStrong" color={colors.roseStrong}>{saving ? 'Đang lưu…' : 'Lưu'}</AppText>
        </Pressable>} />

      <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={{ gap: 10 }}>
        {photos.map((uri) => <PhotoTile key={uri} uri={uri} seed={uri} style={{ width: 110, height: 130 }} />)}
        <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Thêm ảnh" onPress={addPhotos} style={styles.add}>
          <Icon name="camera" size={22} color={colors.roseStrong} />
          <AppText variant="captionStrong" color={colors.roseStrong}>Thêm ảnh</AppText>
        </Pressable>
      </ScrollView>

      <TextField label="Tiêu đề" value={form.title} onChangeText={(title) => set({ title })} placeholder="VD: Valentine đầu tiên" />

      {mode === 'create' ? (
        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 8 }}>
          {TYPES.filter((t) => partner || t === 'CUSTOM').map((t) => (
            <Chip key={t} label={TYPE_LABELS[t]} selected={form.type === t}
              onPress={() => setForm((f) => ({ ...emptyForm(new Date(), t), title: f.title, date: f.date, message: f.message, place: f.place }))} />
          ))}
        </View>
      ) : null}

      {form.type === 'GIFT_OCCASION' ? (
        <>
          <AppText variant="micro" color={colors.textMuted}>DỊP</AppText>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 8 }}>
            {OCCASIONS.map((o) => <Chip key={o.code} label={o.label} selected={form.occasion === o.code} onPress={() => setForm((f) => applyOccasion(f, o.code, new Date()))} />)}
          </View>
          <AppText variant="micro" color={colors.textMuted}>QUÀ CHO AI?</AppText>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8 }}>
            <Chip label="Quà cho mình" selected={form.subject === 'me'} onPress={() => set({ subject: 'me' })} />
            <Chip label={`Quà cho ${partner?.displayName ?? 'người ấy'}`} selected={form.subject === 'partner'} onPress={() => set({ subject: 'partner' })} />
          </View>
        </>
      ) : null}

      <Card style={{ paddingVertical: 4, gap: 0 }}>
        <ListRow testID="row-date" icon="calendar" iconBg={colors.lavender} iconColor={colors.lavenderInk} title="Ngày" subtitle={dateLabel} onPress={() => setSheet('date')} />
        {partner ? (
          <ListRow icon="pin" iconBg={colors.mint} iconColor={colors.mintInk} title="Địa điểm"
            subtitle={form.place?.name ?? existing.data?.place?.name ?? 'Chưa có'} onPress={() => setSheet('place')} />
        ) : null}
        <ListRow icon="repeat" iconBg={colors.peachSoft} iconColor={colors.peachInk} title="Lặp lại" subtitle={repeatSubtitle}
          onPress={options.length ? () => setSheet('repeat') : undefined} />
        <ListRow icon="bell" iconBg={colors.roseSoft} iconColor={colors.rose} title="Nhắc nhở"
          subtitle={mode === 'edit' ? 'Chạm để tuỳ chỉnh' : 'Tuỳ chỉnh sau khi lưu'}
          onPress={mode === 'edit' ? () => router.push(`/moments/${params.id}/reminders`) : undefined} />
      </Card>

      <TextField label="Lời nhắn" value={form.message} onChangeText={(message) => set({ message })} multiline placeholder="Viết vài dòng cho ngày này…" />
      <ErrorText message={error} />
      {mode === 'edit' ? <Button title="Xoá kỷ niệm" variant="danger" icon="trash" onPress={confirmDelete} /> : null}

      <DatePickerSheet visible={sheet === 'date'} title={form.type === 'DATE_PLAN' ? 'Ngày & giờ hẹn' : 'Ngày diễn ra'}
        mode={form.type === 'DATE_PLAN' ? 'datetime' : 'date'} value={form.date} time={form.time}
        maxDate={form.type === 'ANNIVERSARY' ? toIsoDate(new Date()) : undefined}
        onClose={() => setSheet(null)} onConfirm={(date, time) => { set({ date, time: form.type === 'DATE_PLAN' ? time : form.time }); setSheet(null); }} />
      <RepeatSheet visible={sheet === 'repeat'} value={form.recurrence} date={form.date} options={options}
        onChange={(recurrence, date) => set({ recurrence, date })} onClose={() => setSheet(null)} />
      <PlaceSheet visible={sheet === 'place'} value={form.place} onChange={(place) => set({ place })} onClose={() => setSheet(null)} />
    </Screen>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  add: { width: 96, height: 130, borderRadius: 20, borderWidth: 1.5, borderStyle: 'dashed', borderColor: colors.rose, backgroundColor: colors.roseSoft, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 6 },
});
```

```tsx
import { router, useLocalSearchParams } from 'expo-router';
import { Pressable, Share, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { ScrollView } from 'react-native';

import { useMe } from '@/features/account/api';
import { useCouple } from '@/features/couple/api';
import { formatDate, formatDateTime, formatTime, toIsoDate, daysBetween } from '@/lib/format';
import { AppText } from '@/ui/AppText';
import { Card } from '@/ui/Card';
import { Icon } from '@/ui/Icon';
import { IconCircle } from '@/ui/IconCircle';
import { PhotoTile } from '@/ui/PhotoTile';
import { colors } from '@/ui/theme';

import { useMoment, useMomentReminders } from './api';
import { daysLeftLabel, moodLabel, TYPE_LABELS } from './labels';
import { nextOccurrence, recurrenceSummary } from './occurrence';

export default function MomentDetailScreen() {
  const { id } = useLocalSearchParams<{ id: string }>();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const moment = useMoment(id);
  const reminders = useMomentReminders(id);
  const me = useMe();
  const couple = useCouple();
  const m = moment.data;
  if (!m) return <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.bg }} />;

  const today = new Date();
  const next = nextOccurrence(m, today);
  const repeating = m.recurrence !== 'NONE' || m.type === 'ANNIVERSARY';
  const summary = m.type === 'ANNIVERSARY' ? 'Theo mốc ngày yêu' : recurrenceSummary(m.recurrence, m.occursOn);
  const countdown = next && next !== toIsoDate(today) ? daysLeftLabel(-daysBetween(next, today)).toLowerCase() : null;
  const author = m.createdBy === me.data?.id ? me.data?.displayName : couple.data?.partner.displayName;
  const mood = moodLabel(m.details.mood);
  const canEdit = m.type !== 'CYCLE' || m.subjectUserId === me.data?.id;
  const nextReminder = reminders.data?.[0];

  return (
    <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.bg }}>
      <ScrollView>
        <PhotoTile uri={m.media[0]?.url} seed={m.id} style={{ height: 380, borderRadius: 0 }} />
        <View style={styles.sheet}>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
            <View style={styles.typeChip}><AppText variant="micro" color={colors.roseStrong}>{TYPE_LABELS[m.type].toUpperCase()}</AppText></View>
            {m.personal ? <View style={[styles.typeChip, { backgroundColor: colors.lavender }]}><AppText variant="micro" color={colors.lavenderInk}>RIÊNG TƯ</AppText></View> : null}
            {m.type !== 'MOMENT' ? (
              <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted}>{repeating ? summary : 'Một lần'}{countdown ? ` · ${countdown}` : ''}</AppText>
            ) : null}
          </View>
          <AppText variant="display">{m.title}</AppText>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 16, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
            <View style={styles.fact}><Icon name="calendar" size={16} color={colors.textMuted} /><AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted}>{formatDate(m.occursOn)}{m.occursAt ? ` · ${formatTime(m.occursAt)}` : ''}</AppText></View>
            {m.place ? <View style={styles.fact}><Icon name="pin" size={16} color={colors.textMuted} /><AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted}>{m.place.name}</AppText></View> : null}
            {author ? <View style={styles.fact}><Icon name="user" size={16} color={colors.textMuted} /><AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted}>{author} lưu</AppText></View> : null}
          </View>
          {mood && m.type === 'MOMENT' ? <View style={[styles.typeChip, { alignSelf: 'flex-start' }]}><AppText variant="captionStrong" color={colors.roseStrong}>{mood}</AppText></View> : null}
          {m.message ? (
            <View style={styles.quote}>
              <Icon name="quote" size={20} color={colors.rose} />
              <AppText style={{ flex: 1 }}>{m.message}</AppText>
            </View>
          ) : null}
          {m.media.length > 1 ? (
            <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" onPress={() => router.push(`/journal/${m.occursOn}`)}>
              <AppText variant="captionStrong" color={colors.roseStrong}>Xem cả {m.media.length} ảnh</AppText>
            </Pressable>
          ) : null}
          {m.type !== 'MOMENT' ? (
            <Card style={{ gap: 4 }}>
              <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <AppText variant="h3">Nhắc nhở</AppText>
                {canEdit ? (
                  <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Tuỳ chỉnh" onPress={() => router.push(`/moments/${m.id}/reminders`)}>
                    <AppText variant="captionStrong" color={colors.roseStrong}>Tuỳ chỉnh</AppText>
                  </Pressable>
                ) : null}
              </View>
              <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, paddingVertical: 8 }}>
                <IconCircle name="bell" bg={colors.roseSoft} color={colors.rose} size={36} />
                <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                  <AppText variant="bodyStrong">{nextReminder ? nextReminder.body : 'Không có nhắc nào sắp tới cho bạn'}</AppText>
                  {nextReminder ? <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted}>{formatDateTime(nextReminder.fireAt)}</AppText> : null}
                </View>
              </View>
            </Card>
          ) : null}
        </View>
      </ScrollView>
      <View style={[styles.top, { top: insets.top + 8 }]}>
        <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Quay lại" onPress={() => router.back()} style={styles.round}><Icon name="back" size={20} /></Pressable>
        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8 }}>
          <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Chia sẻ" style={styles.round}
            onPress={() => Share.share({ message: `${m.title} · ${formatDate(m.occursOn)}` })}><Icon name="share" size={20} /></Pressable>
          {canEdit ? (
            <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Sửa" style={styles.round} onPress={() => router.push(`/moments/${m.id}/edit`)}><Icon name="edit" size={20} /></Pressable>
          ) : null}
        </View>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  sheet: { marginTop: -30, padding: 24, gap: 14, borderTopLeftRadius: 32, borderTopRightRadius: 32, backgroundColor: colors.bg },
  typeChip: { paddingHorizontal: 10, paddingVertical: 5, borderRadius: 999, backgroundColor: colors.roseSoft },
  fact: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 },
  quote: { flexDirection: 'row', gap: 12, padding: 16, borderRadius: 20, backgroundColor: colors.roseSoft },
  top: { position: 'absolute', left: 20, right: 20, flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between' },
  round: { width: 44, height: 44, borderRadius: 22, backgroundColor: colors.surface, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
});
```

```tsx
import { router, useLocalSearchParams } from 'expo-router';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Pressable, View } from 'react-native';

import { useMe } from '@/features/account/api';
import { useCouple } from '@/features/couple/api';
import { useMoment, useSetReminderConfig } from '@/features/moments/api';
import { TYPE_LABELS } from '@/features/moments/labels';
import { recurrenceSummary } from '@/features/moments/occurrence';
import { errorMessage } from '@/lib/api/client';
import type { ReminderConfig } from '@/lib/api/types';
import { AppText } from '@/ui/AppText';
import { Avatar } from '@/ui/Avatar';
import { Button } from '@/ui/Button';
import { Card } from '@/ui/Card';
import { Chip } from '@/ui/Chip';
import { DatePickerSheet } from '@/ui/DatePickerSheet';
import { ErrorText } from '@/ui/ErrorText';
import { IconCircle } from '@/ui/IconCircle';
import { ListRow } from '@/ui/ListRow';
import { PhotoTile } from '@/ui/PhotoTile';
import { Screen } from '@/ui/Screen';
import { Sheet } from '@/ui/Sheet';
import { TextField } from '@/ui/TextField';
import { colors } from '@/ui/theme';
import { TopBar } from '@/ui/TopBar';

import { Choice, fromAudience, ownerOf, toAudience } from './audience';
import { MAX_OFFSETS, OFFSET_PRESETS, offsetLabel, parseMilestones, toggleOffset } from './offsets';

const QUICK = ['P0D', 'P1D', 'P3D', 'P7D', 'PT2H'];

export default function ReminderConfigScreen() {
  const { id } = useLocalSearchParams<{ id: string }>();
  const moment = useMoment(id);
  const me = useMe();
  const couple = useCouple();
  const save = useSetReminderConfig(id);
  const [choice, setChoice] = useState<Choice>('both');
  const [offsets, setOffsets] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [sendAt, setSendAt] = useState('08:00');
  const [milestones, setMilestones] = useState('');
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
  const [sheet, setSheet] = useState<'time' | 'more' | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!moment.data || !me.data) return;
    const cfg = moment.data.effectiveReminderConfig;
    setChoice(moment.data.personal ? 'me' : fromAudience(cfg.recipients ?? 'BOTH', me.data.id, ownerOf(moment.data)));
    setOffsets(cfg.offsets ?? []);
    setSendAt(cfg.sendAt ?? '08:00');
    setMilestones(cfg.milestoneDays ? cfg.milestoneDays.join(', ') : '');
    setReady(true);
  }, [moment.data, me.data]);

  if (!moment.data || !me.data || !ready) return <Screen><AppText color={colors.textMuted}>Đang tải…</AppText></Screen>;
  const m = moment.data;
  const partnerName = couple.data?.partner.displayName ?? 'người ấy';
  const locked = m.type === 'CYCLE' && m.subjectUserId !== me.data.id;
  const isAnniversary = m.type === 'ANNIVERSARY';

  function toggle(iso: string) {
    const r = toggleOffset(offsets, iso);
    setOffsets(r.list);
    setError(r.error);
  }

  async function submit(config: ReminderConfig | null) {
    setError(null);
    try {
      await save.mutateAsync(config);
      router.back();
    } catch (e) {
      setError(errorMessage(e));
    }
  }

  function onSave() {
    const parsed = isAnniversary && milestones.trim() ? parseMilestones(milestones) : { days: [], error: null };
    if (parsed.error) return setError(parsed.error);
    submit({
      recipients: m.personal ? 'OWNER' : toAudience(choice, me.data!.id, ownerOf(m)),
      offsets,
      sendAt,
      yearly: isAnniversary ? true : null,
      milestoneDays: isAnniversary && milestones.trim() ? parsed.days : null,
    });
  }

  const target = choice === 'me' ? `Gửi cho ${me.data.displayName}` : choice === 'partner' ? `Gửi cho ${partnerName}` : 'Gửi cho cả hai';
  const first = offsets[0];
  const preview = first ? `${first === 'P0D' ? 'Hôm nay là' : `${offsetLabel(first).replace('Trước', 'Còn')} nữa là`} ${m.title} · ${target}` : 'Chưa chọn lần nhắc nào';
  const recipients: { value: Choice; label: string }[] = [{ value: 'me', label: 'Mình' }, { value: 'partner', label: 'Người ấy' }, { value: 'both', label: 'Cả hai' }];

  return (
    <Screen bottomPad={40}>
      <TopBar title="Nhắc nhở" right={locked ? null : (
        <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Lưu" onPress={onSave} hitSlop={8}><AppText variant="bodyStrong" color={colors.roseStrong}>Lưu</AppText></Pressable>
      )} />
      <Card style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12 }}>
        <PhotoTile uri={m.media[0]?.url} seed={m.id} style={{ width: 48, height: 48, borderRadius: 14 }} />
        <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
          <AppText variant="bodyStrong">{m.title}</AppText>
          <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted}>{TYPE_LABELS[m.type]} · {isAnniversary ? 'theo mốc ngày yêu' : recurrenceSummary(m.recurrence, m.occursOn).toLowerCase()}</AppText>
        </View>
      </Card>
      {locked ? <AppText color={colors.textMuted}>Chỉ người theo dõi chu kỳ mới đổi được nhắc nhở này.</AppText> : (
        <>
          {m.personal ? (
            <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted}>Kỷ niệm riêng — chỉ nhắc cho bạn</AppText>
          ) : (
            <>
              <AppText variant="micro" color={colors.textMuted}>AI SẼ NHẬN NHẮC?</AppText>
              <View accessibilityRole="radiogroup" style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 10 }}>
                {recipients.map((r) => {
                  const on = choice === r.value;
                  return (
                    <Pressable key={r.value} accessibilityRole="radio" accessibilityLabel={r.label} accessibilityState={{ checked: on }} onPress={() => setChoice(r.value)}
                      style={{ flex: 1, alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, paddingVertical: 14, borderRadius: 24, borderWidth: 2, borderColor: on ? colors.rose : 'transparent', backgroundColor: on ? colors.roseSoft : colors.surface }}>
                      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row' }}>
                        {r.value !== 'partner' ? <Avatar name={me.data!.displayName} tone="rose" size={28} ring /> : null}
                        {r.value !== 'me' ? <View style={{ marginLeft: r.value === 'both' ? -8 : 0 }}><Avatar name={partnerName} tone="lavender" size={28} ring /></View> : null}
                      </View>
                      <AppText variant="captionStrong" color={on ? colors.roseStrong : colors.text}>{r.label}</AppText>
                    </Pressable>
                  );
                })}
              </View>
            </>
          )}
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
            <AppText variant="micro" color={colors.textMuted}>NHẮC TRƯỚC</AppText>
            <AppText variant="micro" color={colors.roseStrong}>{offsets.length}/{MAX_OFFSETS} đã chọn</AppText>
          </View>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 8 }}>
            {[...new Set([...QUICK, ...offsets])].map((o) => <Chip key={o} label={offsetLabel(o)} selected={offsets.includes(o)} onPress={() => toggle(o)} />)}
            <Chip label="+ Tuỳ chỉnh" selected={false} onPress={() => setSheet('more')} />
          </View>
          <Card style={{ paddingVertical: 4 }}>
            <ListRow icon="clock" iconBg={colors.peachSoft} iconColor={colors.peachInk} title="Giờ gửi" subtitle="Cho các lần nhắc theo ngày"
              right={<AppText variant="bodyStrong" color={colors.roseStrong}>{sendAt}</AppText>} onPress={() => setSheet('time')} />
          </Card>
          {isAnniversary ? (
            <TextField label="Mốc ngày (cách nhau bằng dấu phẩy)" value={milestones} onChangeText={setMilestones} placeholder="100, 200, 365, 520, 1000" keyboardType="numbers-and-punctuation" />
          ) : null}
          <AppText variant="micro" color={colors.textMuted}>XEM TRƯỚC THÔNG BÁO</AppText>
          <Card style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12 }}>
            <IconCircle name="heartFilled" bg={colors.roseStrong} color={colors.white} size={40} />
            <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              <AppText variant="bodyStrong">{m.title} sắp tới</AppText>
              <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted}>{preview}</AppText>
            </View>
          </Card>
          <ErrorText message={error} />
          <Button title="Khôi phục mặc định" variant="ghost" onPress={() => submit(null)} />
        </>
      )}
      <DatePickerSheet visible={sheet === 'time'} mode="time" title="Giờ gửi nhắc" value={m.occursOn} time={sendAt}
        onClose={() => setSheet(null)} onConfirm={(_, t) => { if (t) setSendAt(t); setSheet(null); }} />
      <Sheet visible={sheet === 'more'} onClose={() => setSheet(null)} title="Nhắc trước">
        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 8 }}>
          {OFFSET_PRESETS.map((o) => <Chip key={o} label={offsetLabel(o)} selected={offsets.includes(o)} onPress={() => toggle(o)} />)}
        </View>
        <Button title="Xong" onPress={() => setSheet(null)} />
      </Sheet>
    </Screen>
  );
}
```

> Câu xem trước: offset đầu tiên trong danh sách đã sắp (xa nhất) → `'P7D'` cho ra "Còn 7 ngày nữa là Valentine · Gửi cho Minh" như test kỳ vọng.

`src/features/journal/__tests__/group-test.ts`:

```ts
import { countByDay, dayLabel, groupByDay, journalMoments, step } from '@/features/journal/group';
import type { Moment } from '@/lib/api/types';

function m(id: string, occursOn: string, extra: Partial<Moment> = {}): Moment {
  return {
    id, type: 'MOMENT', title: id, occursOn, occursAt: null, recurrence: 'NONE', subjectUserId: null, message: null,
    details: {}, reminderConfig: null, effectiveReminderConfig: { milestoneDays: null, yearly: null, offsets: [], sendAt: '08:00', recipients: 'OWNER' },
    place: null, media: [], createdBy: 'u1', createdAt: `${occursOn}T0${id.length}:00:00Z`, updatedAt: '', personal: false, ...extra,
  };
}
const photo = (id: string) => ({ id, url: `https://x/${id}.jpg`, width: 1, height: 1, filter: null, takenAt: null });

test('journal keeps past non-cycle moments matching the filter', () => {
  const list = [m('a', '2026-10-10'), m('b', '2026-10-20'), m('c', '2026-10-05', { type: 'CYCLE' }), m('d', '2026-10-08', { type: 'DATE_PLAN' })];
  expect(journalMoments(list, '2026-10-11').map((x) => x.id)).toEqual(['a', 'd']);
  expect(journalMoments(list, '2026-10-11', 'DATE').map((x) => x.id)).toEqual(['d']);
});

test('groups by day newest first and counts per day', () => {
  const list = [m('a', '2026-10-08'), m('bb', '2026-10-10'), m('c', '2026-10-10')];
  const groups = groupByDay(list);
  expect(groups.map((g) => g.date)).toEqual(['2026-10-10', '2026-10-08']);
  expect(groups[0].moments.map((x) => x.id)).toEqual(['c', 'bb']);
  expect(countByDay(list)).toEqual({ '2026-10-08': 1, '2026-10-10': 2 });
  expect(dayLabel('2026-10-10')).toBe('Thứ Bảy, 10/10');
});

test('stepping goes through photos then moments, text moments count as one slide', () => {
  const items = [m('a', '2026-10-10', { media: [photo('1'), photo('2')] }), m('b', '2026-10-10')];
  expect(step(items, { item: 0, photo: 0 }, 1)).toEqual({ item: 0, photo: 1 });
  expect(step(items, { item: 0, photo: 1 }, 1)).toEqual({ item: 1, photo: 0 });
  expect(step(items, { item: 1, photo: 0 }, 1)).toEqual({ item: 1, photo: 0 });
  expect(step(items, { item: 1, photo: 0 }, -1)).toEqual({ item: 0, photo: 0 });
  expect(step(items, { item: 0, photo: 1 }, -1)).toEqual({ item: 0, photo: 0 });
});
```

```tsx
import { fireEvent, screen } from '@testing-library/react-native';
import { router, useLocalSearchParams } from 'expo-router';

import JournalScreen from '@/features/journal/JournalScreen';
import { toIsoDate } from '@/lib/format';
import { mockApi } from '@/test/api-mock';
import { renderWithProviders } from '@/test/render';

const today = new Date();
const iso = (offset: number) => { const d = new Date(today); d.setDate(d.getDate() - offset); return toIsoDate(d); };
const base = { occursAt: null, recurrence: 'NONE', subjectUserId: null, message: null, reminderConfig: null, effectiveReminderConfig: {}, place: null, createdBy: 'u1', updatedAt: '', personal: false };
const photo = (id: string) => ({ id, url: `https://x/${id}.jpg`, width: 1, height: 1, filter: null, takenAt: null });
const LIST = [
  { ...base, id: 'a', type: 'MOMENT', title: 'Cà phê', occursOn: iso(1), details: { mood: 'MISSING' }, media: [photo('1'), photo('2'), photo('3')], createdAt: `${iso(1)}T01:00:00Z` },
  { ...base, id: 'b', type: 'MOMENT', title: 'Chỉ chữ thôi', occursOn: iso(1), details: {}, media: [], createdAt: `${iso(1)}T02:00:00Z` },
  { ...base, id: 'c', type: 'DATE_PLAN', title: 'Xem phim', occursOn: iso(3), details: {}, media: [photo('4')], createdAt: `${iso(3)}T01:00:00Z` },
];

describe('JournalScreen', () => {
  beforeEach(() => mockApi({ 'GET /api/moments': { body: LIST } }));

  test('grid groups by day, shows counts and opens a day', async () => {
    (useLocalSearchParams as jest.Mock).mockReturnValue({});
    await renderWithProviders(<JournalScreen />);
    expect(await screen.findByText('2 kỷ niệm')).toBeOnTheScreen();
    expect(screen.getByText('Chỉ chữ thôi')).toBeOnTheScreen(); // khoảnh khắc chỉ chữ hiện thẻ chữ
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: `Xem 2 kỷ niệm ngày ${iso(1)}` }));
    expect(router.push).toHaveBeenCalledWith(`/journal/${iso(1)}`);
  });

  test('filter chips narrow the grid', async () => {
    (useLocalSearchParams as jest.Mock).mockReturnValue({});
    await renderWithProviders(<JournalScreen />);
    await fireEvent.press(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Hẹn hò' }));
    expect(screen.queryByText('Chỉ chữ thôi')).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.getByText('1 kỷ niệm')).toBeOnTheScreen();
  });

  test('month view marks days and lists the selected day', async () => {
    (useLocalSearchParams as jest.Mock).mockReturnValue({ view: 'month' });
    await renderWithProviders(<JournalScreen />);
    const day = Number(iso(3).slice(8));
    await fireEvent.press(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: `Ngày ${day}, 1 kỷ niệm` }));
    expect(screen.getByText('Xem phim')).toBeOnTheScreen();
  });

  test('switching tabs updates the view param', async () => {
    (useLocalSearchParams as jest.Mock).mockReturnValue({});
    await renderWithProviders(<JournalScreen />);
    await fireEvent.press(await screen.findByRole('tab', { name: 'Năm' }));
    expect(router.setParams).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ view: 'year' });
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { fireEvent, screen } from '@testing-library/react-native';
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `group.ts`**

```ts
import type { Moment, MomentType } from '@/lib/api/types';
import { dayMonth, weekdayName } from '@/lib/calendar';

export type JournalFilter = 'ALL' | 'MOMENT' | 'MILESTONE' | 'DATE' | 'GIFT';
export type DayGroup = { date: string; moments: Moment[] };
export type Position = { item: number; photo: number };

const FILTER_TYPES: Record<Exclude<JournalFilter, 'ALL'>, MomentType[]> = {
  MOMENT: ['MOMENT'],
  MILESTONE: ['ANNIVERSARY', 'CUSTOM'],
  DATE: ['DATE_PLAN'],
  GIFT: ['GIFT_OCCASION'],
};

/** Nhật ký chỉ gồm kỷ niệm đã diễn ra (≤ hôm nay), không gồm chu kỳ. */
export function journalMoments(list: Moment[], todayIso: string, filter: JournalFilter = 'ALL'): Moment[] {
  return list.filter((m) => m.type !== 'CYCLE' && m.occursOn <= todayIso && (filter === 'ALL' || FILTER_TYPES[filter].includes(m.type)));
}

export function groupByDay(list: Moment[]): DayGroup[] {
  const map = new Map<string, Moment[]>();
  for (const m of list) map.set(m.occursOn, [...(map.get(m.occursOn) ?? []), m]);
  return [...map.entries()]
    .sort(([a], [b]) => b.localeCompare(a))
    .map(([date, moments]) => ({ date, moments: [...moments].sort((x, y) => x.createdAt.localeCompare(y.createdAt)) }));
}

export function countByDay(list: Moment[]): Record<string, number> {
  const out: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const m of list) out[m.occursOn] = (out[m.occursOn] ?? 0) + 1;
  return out;
}

export function dayLabel(iso: string): string {
  return `${weekdayName(iso)}, ${dayMonth(iso)}`;
}

/** Như story: qua hết ảnh của một kỷ niệm rồi mới sang kỷ niệm kế; kỷ niệm không ảnh tính là một trang. */
export function step(items: Moment[], pos: Position, dir: 1 | -1): Position {
  const photos = (i: number) => Math.max(1, items[i].media.length);
  if (dir === 1) {
    if (pos.photo < photos(pos.item) - 1) return { item: pos.item, photo: pos.photo + 1 };
    if (pos.item < items.length - 1) return { item: pos.item + 1, photo: 0 };
    return pos;
  }
  if (pos.photo > 0) return { item: pos.item, photo: pos.photo - 1 };
  if (pos.item > 0) return { item: pos.item - 1, photo: 0 };
  return pos;
}
```
