```tsx
// src/features/journal/JournalScreen.tsx
import { router, useLocalSearchParams } from 'expo-router';
import { View } from 'react-native';

import { useMoments } from '@/features/moments/api';
import { toIsoDate } from '@/lib/format';
import { AppText } from '@/ui/AppText';
import { Screen } from '@/ui/Screen';
import { SegmentedTabs } from '@/ui/SegmentedTabs';
import { colors } from '@/ui/theme';

import { journalMoments } from './group';
import { JournalGrid } from './JournalGrid';
import { JournalMonth } from './JournalMonth';
import { JournalYear } from './JournalYear';

type View_ = 'grid' | 'month' | 'year';

export default function JournalScreen() {
  const params = useLocalSearchParams<{ view?: View_; ym?: string }>();
  const view: View_ = params.view === 'month' || params.view === 'year' ? params.view : 'grid';
  const moments = useMoments();
  const list = journalMoments(moments.data ?? [], toIsoDate(new Date()));
  return (
    <Screen gap={14}>
      <View>
        <AppText variant="display">Nhật ký</AppText>
        <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted}>{list.length} kỷ niệm</AppText>
      </View>
      <SegmentedTabs options={[{ key: 'grid', label: 'Lưới' }, { key: 'month', label: 'Tháng' }, { key: 'year', label: 'Năm' }]}
        value={view} onChange={(v) => router.setParams({ view: v })} />
      {view === 'grid' ? <JournalGrid moments={moments.data ?? []} /> : null}
      {view === 'month' ? <JournalMonth moments={moments.data ?? []} initial={params.ym} /> : null}
      {view === 'year' ? <JournalYear moments={moments.data ?? []} /> : null}
    </Screen>
  );
}
```

```tsx
// src/features/journal/JournalGrid.tsx
import { router } from 'expo-router';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Pressable, ScrollView, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';

import { moodLabel, TYPE_LABELS } from '@/features/moments/labels';
import type { Moment } from '@/lib/api/types';
import { toIsoDate } from '@/lib/format';
import { AppText } from '@/ui/AppText';
import { Chip } from '@/ui/Chip';
import { Icon } from '@/ui/Icon';
import { PhotoTile } from '@/ui/PhotoTile';
import { colors } from '@/ui/theme';

import { dayLabel, groupByDay, JournalFilter, journalMoments } from './group';

const FILTERS: { key: JournalFilter; label: string }[] = [
  { key: 'ALL', label: 'Tất cả' }, { key: 'MOMENT', label: 'Khoảnh khắc' }, { key: 'MILESTONE', label: 'Kỷ niệm' },
  { key: 'DATE', label: 'Hẹn hò' }, { key: 'GIFT', label: 'Quà' },
];

function Tile({ moment, style, overlay }: { moment: Moment; style: object; overlay?: string }) {
  const media = moment.media[0];
  if (!media) {
    return (
      <View style={[style, styles.textTile]}>
        <Icon name="quote" size={18} color={colors.lavenderInk} />
        <AppText variant="captionStrong" numberOfLines={3}>{moment.title}</AppText>
      </View>
    );
  }
  return (
    <PhotoTile uri={media.url} seed={moment.id} style={style}>
      {overlay ? <View style={styles.more}><AppText variant="h2" color={colors.white}>{overlay}</AppText></View> : null}
    </PhotoTile>
  );
}

export function JournalGrid({ moments }: { moments: Moment[] }) {
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<JournalFilter>('ALL');
  const groups = groupByDay(journalMoments(moments, toIsoDate(new Date()), filter));
  return (
    <>
      <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={{ gap: 8 }}>
        {FILTERS.map((f) => <Chip key={f.key} label={f.label} selected={filter === f.key} onPress={() => setFilter(f.key)} />)}
      </ScrollView>
      {groups.length === 0 ? <AppText color={colors.textMuted}>Chưa có kỷ niệm nào. Bấm + để lưu khoảnh khắc đầu tiên.</AppText> : null}
      {groups.map(({ date, moments: day }) => {
        const first = day[0];
        const photos = day.length;
        const label = TYPE_LABELS[first.type] + (moodLabel(first.details.mood) ? ` · ${moodLabel(first.details.mood)}` : '');
        return (
          <View key={date} style={{ gap: 8 }}>
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'baseline' }}>
              <AppText variant="bodyStrong">{dayLabel(date)}</AppText>
              <AppText variant="captionStrong" color={photos > 1 ? colors.roseStrong : colors.textMuted}>{photos} kỷ niệm</AppText>
            </View>
            <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel={`Xem ${photos} kỷ niệm ngày ${date}`} onPress={() => router.push(`/journal/${date}`)}>
              {day.length === 1 ? (
                <Tile moment={first} style={{ height: 150, borderRadius: 20 }} />
              ) : day.length === 2 ? (
                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 6, height: 160 }}>
                  <Tile moment={day[0]} style={{ flex: 1, borderRadius: 20 }} />
                  <Tile moment={day[1]} style={{ flex: 1, borderRadius: 20 }} />
                </View>
              ) : (
                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 6, height: 204 }}>
                  <View style={{ flex: 2 }}>
                    <Tile moment={day[0]} style={{ flex: 1, borderRadius: 20 }} />
                    <View style={styles.badge}><AppText variant="captionStrong" color={colors.roseStrong} style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>{label}</AppText></View>
                  </View>
                  <View style={{ flex: 1, gap: 6 }}>
                    <Tile moment={day[1]} style={{ flex: 1, borderRadius: 16 }} />
                    <Tile moment={day[2]} style={{ flex: 1, borderRadius: 16 }} overlay={day.length > 3 ? `+${day.length - 3}` : undefined} />
                  </View>
                </View>
              )}
            </Pressable>
          </View>
        );
      })}
    </>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  textTile: { backgroundColor: colors.lavender, padding: 12, gap: 6, justifyContent: 'center', overflow: 'hidden' },
  more: { position: 'absolute', top: 0, left: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, backgroundColor: 'rgba(43,26,46,0.5)', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  badge: { position: 'absolute', left: 10, bottom: 10, paddingHorizontal: 10, paddingVertical: 4, borderRadius: 999, backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.9)' },
});
```

- [ ] **Step 6: `JournalMonth.tsx` & `JournalYear.tsx`**

```tsx
// src/features/journal/JournalMonth.tsx
import { router } from 'expo-router';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Pressable, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';

import { captureStore } from '@/features/create/capture-store';
import type { Moment } from '@/lib/api/types';
import { monthGrid, monthLabel, shiftMonth, WEEKDAYS } from '@/lib/calendar';
import { toIsoDate } from '@/lib/format';
import { AppText } from '@/ui/AppText';
import { Card } from '@/ui/Card';
import { Icon } from '@/ui/Icon';
import { PhotoTile } from '@/ui/PhotoTile';
import { colors, gradientFor } from '@/ui/theme';

import { countByDay, dayLabel, journalMoments } from './group';

export function JournalMonth({ moments, initial }: { moments: Moment[]; initial?: string }) {
  const todayIso = toIsoDate(new Date());
  const start = initial && /^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(initial) ? initial : todayIso.slice(0, 7);
  const [view, setView] = useState({ year: Number(start.slice(0, 4)), month: Number(start.slice(5, 7)) });
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string>(todayIso);
  const list = journalMoments(moments, todayIso);
  const counts = countByDay(list);
  const birthdays = new Set(moments.filter((m) => m.type === 'GIFT_OCCASION').map((m) => m.occursOn.slice(5)));
  const dayMoments = list.filter((m) => m.occursOn === selected);

  return (
    <>
      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
        <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Tháng trước" style={styles.arrow} onPress={() => setView(shiftMonth(view.year, view.month, -1))}><Icon name="back" size={18} /></Pressable>
        <AppText variant="h2">{monthLabel(view.year, view.month)}</AppText>
        <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Tháng sau" style={styles.arrow} onPress={() => setView(shiftMonth(view.year, view.month, 1))}><Icon name="chevronRight" size={18} /></Pressable>
      </View>
      <Card style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', paddingHorizontal: 8, gap: 0 }}>
        {WEEKDAYS.map((w) => <AppText key={w} variant="micro" color={w === 'CN' ? colors.roseStrong : colors.textMuted} style={styles.cellW}>{w}</AppText>)}
        {monthGrid(view.year, view.month).map((c, i) => {
          if (!c.iso) return <View key={`e${i}`} style={styles.cell} />;
          const n = counts[c.iso] ?? 0;
          const gift = birthdays.has(c.iso.slice(5));
          const on = c.iso === selected;
          const cover = list.find((m) => m.occursOn === c.iso && m.media[0])?.media[0];
          return (
            <View key={c.iso} style={styles.cell}>
              <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel={`Ngày ${c.day}${n ? `, ${n} kỷ niệm` : ''}`} accessibilityState={{ selected: on }}
                onPress={() => setSelected(c.iso!)}
                style={[styles.day, gift && { backgroundColor: colors.peachSoft }, n > 0 && !cover && { backgroundColor: gradientFor(c.iso)[0] },
                  on && { borderWidth: 2.5, borderColor: colors.roseStrong }, c.iso === todayIso && !on && { borderWidth: 1.5, borderStyle: 'dashed', borderColor: colors.roseStrong }]}>
                {cover ? <PhotoTile uri={cover.url} seed={c.iso} style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} /> : null}
                <AppText variant={n ? 'bodyStrong' : 'body'} color={c.weekday === 6 && !n ? colors.roseStrong : colors.text}>{c.day}</AppText>
                {n > 1 ? <AppText variant="micro" style={{ letterSpacing: 0 }}>{'•'.repeat(Math.min(n, 3))}</AppText> : null}
              </Pressable>
            </View>
          );
        })}
      </Card>
      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'baseline' }}>
        <AppText variant="h3">{dayLabel(selected)}</AppText>
        <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" onPress={() => { captureStore.reset({ date: selected }); router.push('/create'); }}>
          <AppText variant="captionStrong" color={colors.roseStrong}>+ Thêm vào ngày này</AppText>
        </Pressable>
      </View>
      {dayMoments.length === 0 ? (
        <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted} style={styles.empty}>Chưa có kỷ niệm nào ngày này</AppText>
      ) : dayMoments.map((m) => (
        <Pressable key={m.id} accessibilityRole="button" onPress={() => router.push(`/journal/${selected}`)}>
          <Card style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12 }}>
            <PhotoTile uri={m.media[0]?.url} seed={m.id} style={{ width: 52, height: 52, borderRadius: 14 }} />
            <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              <AppText variant="bodyStrong">{m.title}</AppText>
              <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted}>{m.media.length} ảnh</AppText>
            </View>
            <Icon name="chevronRight" size={18} color={colors.textMuted} />
          </Card>
        </Pressable>
      ))}
    </>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  arrow: { width: 40, height: 40, borderRadius: 20, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.line, backgroundColor: colors.surface, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  cellW: { width: `${100 / 7}%`, textAlign: 'center', paddingBottom: 6 },
  cell: { width: `${100 / 7}%`, height: 50, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  day: { width: 40, height: 46, borderRadius: 14, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', overflow: 'hidden' },
  empty: { padding: 16, borderRadius: 20, borderWidth: 1.5, borderStyle: 'dashed', borderColor: colors.line, textAlign: 'center' },
});
```

> `captureStore.reset({ date })` rồi mở camera: camera hiện đặt `date` bằng hôm nay khi chụp (`start()` trong `CameraScreen`) — sửa `start()` để giữ `captureStore.get().date` nếu đã có: `date: captureStore.get().date || toIsoDate(new Date())`.

```tsx
// src/features/journal/JournalYear.tsx
import { router } from 'expo-router';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Pressable, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';

import type { Moment } from '@/lib/api/types';
import { monthGrid } from '@/lib/calendar';
import { pad, toIsoDate } from '@/lib/format';
import { AppText } from '@/ui/AppText';
import { Icon } from '@/ui/Icon';
import { colors, shadow } from '@/ui/theme';

import { countByDay, journalMoments } from './group';

export function JournalYear({ moments }: { moments: Moment[] }) {
  const today = new Date();
  const [year, setYear] = useState(today.getFullYear());
  const list = journalMoments(moments, toIsoDate(today));
  const counts = countByDay(list);
  const yearTotal = list.filter((m) => m.occursOn.startsWith(String(year))).length;
  return (
    <>
      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
        <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Năm trước" style={styles.arrow} onPress={() => setYear(year - 1)}><Icon name="back" size={18} /></Pressable>
        <View style={{ alignItems: 'center' }}>
          <AppText variant="h2">{year}</AppText>
          <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted}>{yearTotal} kỷ niệm</AppText>
        </View>
        <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Năm sau" style={styles.arrow} onPress={() => setYear(year + 1)}><Icon name="chevronRight" size={18} /></Pressable>
      </View>
      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 10 }}>
        {Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, i) => i + 1).map((month) => {
          const key = `${year}-${pad(month)}`;
          const n = list.filter((m) => m.occursOn.startsWith(key)).length;
          const current = year === today.getFullYear() && month === today.getMonth() + 1;
          return (
            <Pressable key={key} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel={`Tháng ${month}, ${n} kỷ niệm`}
              onPress={() => router.setParams({ view: 'month', ym: key })}
              style={[styles.card, current && { borderColor: colors.rose }]}>
              <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
                <AppText variant="bodyStrong" color={current ? colors.roseStrong : colors.text}>Th {month}</AppText>
                <AppText variant="micro" color={colors.textMuted}>{n}</AppText>
              </View>
              <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 3 }}>
                {monthGrid(year, month).map((c, i) => (
                  <View key={i} style={{ width: 9, height: 9, borderRadius: 3, backgroundColor: !c.iso ? 'transparent' : counts[c.iso] ? (counts[c.iso] > 1 ? colors.rose : colors.roseBorder) : colors.line }} />
                ))}
              </View>
            </Pressable>
          );
        })}
      </View>
    </>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  arrow: { width: 40, height: 40, borderRadius: 20, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.line, backgroundColor: colors.surface, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  card: { width: '31%', padding: 10, gap: 6, borderRadius: 20, borderWidth: 2, borderColor: 'transparent', backgroundColor: colors.surface, ...shadow.card },
});
```

- [ ] **Step 7: `DayViewerScreen.tsx` (D4)**

```tsx
import { router, useLocalSearchParams } from 'expo-router';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Pressable, ScrollView, StyleSheet, useWindowDimensions, View } from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';

import { useMe } from '@/features/account/api';
import { useCouple } from '@/features/couple/api';
import { useMoments } from '@/features/moments/api';
import { moodLabel, TYPE_LABELS } from '@/features/moments/labels';
import { weekdayName } from '@/lib/calendar';
import { formatDate, formatTime } from '@/lib/format';
import { AppText } from '@/ui/AppText';
import { Avatar } from '@/ui/Avatar';
import { Icon } from '@/ui/Icon';
import { PhotoTile } from '@/ui/PhotoTile';
import { colors, shadow } from '@/ui/theme';
import { TopBar } from '@/ui/TopBar';

import { Position, step } from './group';

export default function DayViewerScreen() {
  const { date } = useLocalSearchParams<{ date: string }>();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { width } = useWindowDimensions();
  const moments = useMoments();
  const me = useMe();
  const couple = useCouple();
  const [pos, setPos] = useState<Position>({ item: 0, photo: 0 });
  const items = (moments.data ?? []).filter((m) => m.occursOn === date && m.type !== 'CYCLE').sort((a, b) => a.createdAt.localeCompare(b.createdAt));
  const cardW = width - 84;

  if (items.length === 0) {
    return (
      <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.bg, paddingTop: insets.top + 8, paddingHorizontal: 20 }}>
        <TopBar title={date ? `${weekdayName(date)}, ${formatDate(date)}` : ''} />
        <AppText color={colors.textMuted} style={{ marginTop: 24 }}>{moments.isSuccess ? 'Ngày này chưa có kỷ niệm nào.' : 'Đang tải…'}</AppText>
      </View>
    );
  }

  const m = items[Math.min(pos.item, items.length - 1)];
  const media = m.media[pos.photo];
  const mood = moodLabel(m.details.mood);
  const author = m.createdBy === me.data?.id ? me.data?.displayName : couple.data?.partner.displayName ?? '';
  const time = formatTime(m.occursAt ?? m.createdAt);

  return (
    <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.bg, paddingTop: insets.top + 8 }}>
      <View style={{ paddingHorizontal: 20 }}>
        <TopBar title={`${weekdayName(date!)}, ${formatDate(date!)}`}
          right={<View style={styles.counter}><AppText variant="captionStrong" color={colors.roseStrong}>{pos.item + 1} / {items.length}</AppText></View>} />
        <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted} style={{ marginLeft: 56 }}>{items.length} kỷ niệm trong ngày</AppText>
      </View>
      <View style={styles.progress}>
        {items.map((it, i) => <View key={it.id} style={[styles.seg, { backgroundColor: i <= pos.item ? colors.rose : colors.line }]} />)}
      </View>

      <View style={{ height: 400, marginTop: 10, alignItems: 'center' }}>
        <View style={[{ width: cardW, height: 400, borderRadius: 28, overflow: 'hidden' }, shadow.raised]}>
          {media ? (
            <PhotoTile uri={media.url} seed={m.id} style={{ flex: 1, borderRadius: 0 }} />
          ) : (
            <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.lavender, padding: 28, justifyContent: 'center', gap: 14 }}>
              <Icon name="quote" size={34} color={colors.lavenderInk} />
              <AppText variant="h2">{m.message || m.title}</AppText>
            </View>
          )}
          <View style={styles.typeChip}><AppText variant="captionStrong" color={colors.roseStrong} style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>{TYPE_LABELS[m.type]}{mood ? ` · ${mood}` : ''}</AppText></View>
          {m.media.length > 1 ? <View style={styles.photoCount}><AppText variant="micro" color={colors.white} style={{ letterSpacing: 0 }}>Ảnh {pos.photo + 1}/{m.media.length}</AppText></View> : null}
          <View style={styles.who}>
            <Avatar name={author || '?'} tone={m.createdBy === me.data?.id ? 'rose' : 'lavender'} size={26} />
            <AppText variant="captionStrong">{author} · {time}</AppText>
          </View>
          <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Ảnh hoặc kỷ niệm trước" onPress={() => setPos(step(items, pos, -1))} style={[StyleSheet.absoluteFill, { right: '60%' }]} />
          <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Ảnh hoặc kỷ niệm tiếp theo" onPress={() => setPos(step(items, pos, 1))} style={[StyleSheet.absoluteFill, { left: '40%' }]} />
        </View>
      </View>

      <View style={{ paddingHorizontal: 24, gap: 6, marginTop: 14 }}>
        {media && m.message ? <AppText variant="bodyStrong">{m.message}</AppText> : media ? <AppText variant="bodyStrong">{m.title}</AppText> : null}
        {m.place ? (
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
            <Icon name="pin" size={14} color={colors.textMuted} />
            <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted}>{m.place.name}</AppText>
          </View>
        ) : null}
        {m.type !== 'MOMENT' ? (
          <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" onPress={() => router.push(`/moments/${m.id}`)}>
            <AppText variant="captionStrong" color={colors.roseStrong}>Xem chi tiết & nhắc nhở</AppText>
          </Pressable>
        ) : null}
      </View>

      <AppText variant="micro" color={colors.textMuted} style={{ marginHorizontal: 24, marginTop: 16 }}>TRONG NGÀY NÀY</AppText>
      <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={{ paddingHorizontal: 24, gap: 10, paddingVertical: 8 }}>
        {items.map((it, i) => (
          <Pressable key={it.id} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel={`Mở kỷ niệm ${i + 1}: ${it.title}`} accessibilityState={{ selected: i === pos.item }}
            onPress={() => setPos({ item: i, photo: 0 })} style={{ alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
            {it.media[0] ? (
              <PhotoTile uri={it.media[0].url} seed={it.id} style={[styles.thumb, i === pos.item ? styles.thumbOn : { opacity: 0.7 }]} />
            ) : (
              <View style={[styles.thumb, { backgroundColor: colors.lavender, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }, i === pos.item ? styles.thumbOn : { opacity: 0.7 }]}>
                <Icon name="quote" size={20} color={colors.lavenderInk} />
              </View>
            )}
            <AppText variant="micro" color={i === pos.item ? colors.roseStrong : colors.textMuted} style={{ letterSpacing: 0 }}>{TYPE_LABELS[it.type]}</AppText>
          </Pressable>
        ))}
      </ScrollView>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  counter: { paddingHorizontal: 12, paddingVertical: 6, borderRadius: 999, backgroundColor: colors.roseSoft },
  progress: { flexDirection: 'row', gap: 4, paddingHorizontal: 24, marginTop: 10 },
  seg: { flex: 1, height: 4, borderRadius: 2 },
  typeChip: { position: 'absolute', left: 14, top: 14, paddingHorizontal: 11, paddingVertical: 5, borderRadius: 999, backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.92)' },
  photoCount: { position: 'absolute', right: 14, top: 16, paddingHorizontal: 9, paddingVertical: 4, borderRadius: 999, backgroundColor: 'rgba(43,26,46,0.55)' },
  who: { position: 'absolute', left: 14, bottom: 14, flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, paddingVertical: 5, paddingLeft: 5, paddingRight: 12, borderRadius: 999, backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.92)' },
  thumb: { width: 58, height: 58, borderRadius: 16 },
  thumbOn: { borderWidth: 3, borderColor: colors.rose },
});
```

> Trong test, tiêu đề được so với `'Thứ Bảy, 10/10/2026'` → `formatDate('2026-10-10')` = `'10/10/2026'`. Vuốt ngang: hai vùng chạm trái/phải + dải thumbnail đáp ứng "lướt để xem hết"; nếu muốn cử chỉ vuốt thật, bọc thẻ bằng `PanResponder` (dx < −40 → `step(+1)`, dx > 40 → `step(−1)`) — thêm ở bước làm đẹp, không bắt buộc cho test.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `useCouple`, `useMe`, `useTraits`, `useCatalog`, `useSaveSize`, `useDeleteSize`, `useSavePreference`, `useDeletePreference` (`features/traits/api.ts`), `usePrediction(userId, enabled)` (`features/cycle/api.ts`), `canEdit`, `catalogLabel` (`features/traits/editing.ts`), `uploadImage`, `daysUntilBirthday` (Task 7), `ConnectCard` (Task 6), primitives.
- Produces: `storeLabel(url)` → `'Shopee' | 'Lazada' | 'Tiki' | 'TikTok Shop' | <tên miền>`; `SizeSheet({visible, subjectId, subjectName, entry?, onClose})`; `FavoriteSheet({visible, subjectId, subjectName, entry?, onClose})`; `HobbySheet({visible, subjectId, onClose})`; `PartnerScreen` (default export). Mục "Sở thích khác" = preference `category === 'HOBBY'`. Không hiển thị/nhập giá (spec §12.6).

```tsx
import { fireEvent, screen } from '@testing-library/react-native';
import { Linking } from 'react-native';

import PartnerScreen from '@/features/partner/PartnerScreen';
import { mockApi } from '@/test/api-mock';
import { renderWithProviders } from '@/test/render';

const ME = { id: 'u1', email: '', displayName: 'Lan', handle: 'lan', birthday: null, timezone: 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh', avatarUrl: null };
const NOT_PAIRED = { status: 409, body: { code: 'COUPLE_REQUIRED', detail: 'Bạn chưa ghép đôi' } };
const soon = new Date();
soon.setDate(soon.getDate() + 7);
const bday = `1999-${String(soon.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(soon.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
const COUPLE = { id: 'c1', startedDatingOn: null, createdAt: '', partner: { id: 'u2', displayName: 'Minh', handle: 'minh', birthday: bday, avatarUrl: null } };
const CATALOG = {
  sizeKinds: [{ code: 'SHIRT', label: 'Áo' }, { code: 'PANTS', label: 'Quần' }, { code: 'SHOE', label: 'Giày' }],
  preferenceCategories: [{ code: 'KEYBOARD', label: 'Bàn phím' }, { code: 'WATCH', label: 'Đồng hồ' }, { code: 'HOBBY', label: 'Sở thích' }],
};
const TRAITS = {
  sizes: [{ id: 's1', subjectUserId: 'u2', kind: 'SHIRT', value: 'L', note: 'form rộng', visibility: 'SHARED', createdBy: 'u2', updatedAt: '' }],
  preferences: [
    { id: 'p1', subjectUserId: 'u2', category: 'KEYBOARD', brand: 'Keychron', name: 'Bàn phím K2 Pro', color: null, note: null, visibility: 'SHARED', createdBy: 'u2', updatedAt: '', links: [{ id: 'l1', url: 'https://shopee.vn/k2', label: null, price: 2500000 }], image: null },
    { id: 'p2', subjectUserId: 'u2', category: 'WATCH', brand: 'Casio', name: 'Đồng hồ vintage', color: null, note: null, visibility: 'AUTHOR_ONLY', createdBy: 'u1', updatedAt: '', links: [], image: null },
    { id: 'p3', subjectUserId: 'u2', category: 'HOBBY', brand: null, name: 'Cà phê đen ít đường', color: null, note: null, visibility: 'SHARED', createdBy: 'u2', updatedAt: '', links: [], image: null },
  ],
};

function routes(extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  return {
    'GET /api/me': { body: ME },
    'GET /api/couples/me': { body: COUPLE },
    'GET /api/traits/catalog': { body: CATALOG },
    'GET /api/traits/u2': { body: TRAITS },
    'GET /api/cycles/prediction': { status: 404, body: { code: 'NOT_FOUND', detail: 'x' } },
    ...extra,
  };
}

describe('PartnerScreen', () => {
  test('shows profile, sizes, favorites without price, secret badge and hobbies', async () => {
    const open = jest.spyOn(Linking, 'openURL').mockResolvedValue(true);
    mockApi(routes());
    await renderWithProviders(<PartnerScreen />);
    expect(await screen.findByText('Minh')).toBeOnTheScreen();
    expect(screen.getByText(/còn 7 ngày$/)).toBeOnTheScreen();
    expect(await screen.findByText('form rộng')).toBeOnTheScreen();
    expect(screen.getByText('Bàn phím K2 Pro')).toBeOnTheScreen();
    expect(screen.queryByText(/2\.500\.000|2500000/)).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.getByText('BÍ MẬT')).toBeOnTheScreen();
    expect(screen.getByText('Cà phê đen ít đường')).toBeOnTheScreen();
    expect(screen.queryByText(/ngày nữa là tới ngày của/)).toBeNull();
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'Shopee' }));
    expect(open).toHaveBeenCalledWith('https://shopee.vn/k2');
  });

  test('shows the cycle card only when the partner shares it', async () => {
    const next = new Date();
    next.setDate(next.getDate() + 10);
    const iso = `${next.getFullYear()}-${String(next.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(next.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    mockApi(routes({ 'GET /api/cycles/prediction': { body: { nextStart: iso, averageLength: 28, basedOnCycles: 3 } } }));
    await renderWithProviders(<PartnerScreen />);
    expect(await screen.findByText('Khoảng 10 ngày nữa là tới ngày của Minh')).toBeOnTheScreen();
  });

  test('adds a secret size from the sheet', async () => {
    const api = mockApi(routes({ 'POST /api/traits/sizes': { status: 201, body: {} } }));
    await renderWithProviders(<PartnerScreen />);
    await fireEvent.press(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Thêm size' }));
    expect(screen.getByText('Thêm size cho Minh')).toBeOnTheScreen();
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Giày' }));
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByLabelText('Hoặc nhập size khác'), '42');
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByLabelText('Ghi chú'), 'Nike hơi chật');
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('switch', { name: 'Giữ bí mật' }));
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Lưu size' }));
    await screen.findByText('Minh');
    expect(api.calls.find((c) => c.method === 'POST')?.body).toEqual({ subjectUserId: 'u2', kind: 'SHOE', value: '42', note: 'Nike hơi chật', visibility: 'AUTHOR_ONLY' });
  });

  test('adds a favorite with a shop link and no price', async () => {
    const api = mockApi(routes({ 'POST /api/traits/preferences': { status: 201, body: {} } }));
    await renderWithProviders(<PartnerScreen />);
    await fireEvent.press(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Thêm món thích' }));
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Đồng hồ' }));
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByLabelText('Hãng'), 'Casio');
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByLabelText('Tên món'), 'Đồng hồ vàng');
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByLabelText('Link mua hàng 1'), 'https://shopee.vn/casio');
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Lưu món này' }));
    await screen.findByText('Minh');
    expect(api.calls.find((c) => c.method === 'POST')?.body).toEqual({
      subjectUserId: 'u2', category: 'WATCH', brand: 'Casio', name: 'Đồng hồ vàng', visibility: 'SHARED',
      links: [{ url: 'https://shopee.vn/casio', label: 'Shopee' }],
    });
  });

  test('unpaired user sees the connect card', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'GET /api/me': { body: ME }, 'GET /api/couples/me': NOT_PAIRED });
    await renderWithProviders(<PartnerScreen />);
    expect(await screen.findByText('Kết nối với người ấy')).toBeOnTheScreen();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `links.ts`**

```ts
const STORES: [RegExp, string][] = [
  [/(^|\.)shopee\./, 'Shopee'],
  [/(^|\.)lazada\./, 'Lazada'],
  [/(^|\.)tiki\.vn$/, 'Tiki'],
  [/(^|\.)tiktok\.com$/, 'TikTok Shop'],
];

/** Tên hiển thị cho link mua hàng, suy từ tên miền. */
export function storeLabel(url: string): string {
  const m = /^https?:\/\/([^/]+)/i.exec(url.trim());
  if (!m) return 'Link';
  const host = m[1].toLowerCase().replace(/^www\./, '');
  return STORES.find(([re]) => re.test(host))?.[1] ?? host;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `SizeSheet.tsx` (E2)**

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Pressable, View } from 'react-native';

import { useCatalog, useDeleteSize, useSaveSize } from '@/features/traits/api';
import { errorMessage } from '@/lib/api/client';
import type { SizeEntry } from '@/lib/api/types';
import { AppText } from '@/ui/AppText';
import { Avatar } from '@/ui/Avatar';
import { Button } from '@/ui/Button';
import { Chip } from '@/ui/Chip';
import { ErrorText } from '@/ui/ErrorText';
import { IconCircle } from '@/ui/IconCircle';
import { Sheet } from '@/ui/Sheet';
import { TextField } from '@/ui/TextField';
import { colors } from '@/ui/theme';
import { Toggle } from '@/ui/Toggle';

const LETTER_SIZES = ['XS', 'S', 'M', 'L', 'XL', 'XXL'];
const LETTER_KINDS = ['SHIRT', 'PANTS', 'BRA'];

type Props = { visible: boolean; subjectId: string; subjectName: string; isSelf?: boolean; entry?: SizeEntry | null; onClose: () => void };

export function SecretRow({ value, onChange, name }: { value: boolean; onChange: (v: boolean) => void; name: string }) {
  return (
    <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, padding: 14, borderRadius: 18, backgroundColor: colors.lavender }}>
      <IconCircle name="lock" bg={colors.surface} color={colors.lavenderInk} size={32} />
      <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
        <AppText variant="bodyStrong">Giữ bí mật</AppText>
        <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted}>Chỉ mình bạn thấy — {name} sẽ không biết</AppText>
      </View>
      <Toggle label="Giữ bí mật" value={value} onValueChange={onChange} />
    </View>
  );
}

export function SizeSheet({ visible, subjectId, subjectName, isSelf, entry, onClose }: Props) {
  const catalog = useCatalog();
  const save = useSaveSize(subjectId);
  const remove = useDeleteSize(subjectId);
  const [kind, setKind] = useState('SHIRT');
  const [letter, setLetter] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [custom, setCustom] = useState('');
  const [note, setNote] = useState('');
  const [secret, setSecret] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!visible) return;
    setKind(entry?.kind ?? 'SHIRT');
    const isLetter = !!entry && LETTER_SIZES.includes(entry.value);
    setLetter(isLetter ? entry!.value : null);
    setCustom(entry && !isLetter ? entry.value : '');
    setNote(entry?.note ?? '');
    setSecret(entry?.visibility === 'AUTHOR_ONLY');
    setError(null);
  }, [visible, entry]);

  async function submit() {
    const value = custom.trim() || letter;
    if (!value) return setError('Chọn hoặc nhập size');
    const body: Record<string, unknown> = { kind, value, note: note.trim() || undefined, visibility: secret ? 'AUTHOR_ONLY' : 'SHARED' };
    if (!entry) body.subjectUserId = subjectId;
    if (body.note === undefined) delete body.note;
    try {
      await save.mutateAsync({ id: entry?.id, body });
      onClose();
    } catch (e) {
      setError(errorMessage(e));
    }
  }

  return (
    <Sheet visible={visible} onClose={onClose} title={entry ? 'Sửa size' : isSelf ? 'Thêm size của tôi' : `Thêm size cho ${subjectName}`}
      subtitle="Để chọn quà vừa vặn" right={<Avatar name={subjectName} tone={isSelf ? 'rose' : 'lavender'} size={32} />}>
      <AppText variant="micro" color={colors.textMuted}>LOẠI</AppText>
      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 8 }}>
        {(catalog.data?.sizeKinds ?? []).map((k) => <Chip key={k.code} label={k.label} selected={kind === k.code} onPress={() => setKind(k.code)} />)}
      </View>
      {LETTER_KINDS.includes(kind) ? (
        <>
          <AppText variant="micro" color={colors.textMuted}>SIZE</AppText>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8 }}>
            {LETTER_SIZES.map((s) => (
              <Pressable key={s} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel={`Size ${s}`} accessibilityState={{ selected: letter === s }}
                onPress={() => { setLetter(s); setCustom(''); }}
                style={{ flex: 1, height: 48, borderRadius: 14, borderWidth: 1.5, borderColor: letter === s ? colors.roseStrong : colors.line, backgroundColor: letter === s ? colors.roseStrong : colors.surface, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
                <AppText variant="bodyStrong" color={letter === s ? colors.white : colors.text}>{s}</AppText>
              </Pressable>
            ))}
          </View>
        </>
      ) : null}
      <TextField label="Hoặc nhập size khác" value={custom} onChangeText={setCustom} placeholder="VD: 170/88A, 42.5…" />
      <TextField label="Ghi chú" value={note} onChangeText={setNote} placeholder="Thích form rộng, cổ tròn" />
      {!isSelf ? <SecretRow value={secret} onChange={setSecret} name={subjectName} /> : null}
      <ErrorText message={error} />
      <Button title="Lưu size" onPress={submit} loading={save.isPending} />
      {entry ? <Button title="Xoá" variant="danger" onPress={async () => { await remove.mutateAsync(entry.id); onClose(); }} /> : null}
    </Sheet>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: `FavoriteSheet.tsx` (E3) & `HobbySheet.tsx`**

```tsx
// src/features/partner/FavoriteSheet.tsx
import * as ImagePicker from 'expo-image-picker';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Pressable, View } from 'react-native';

import { uploadImage } from '@/features/media/upload';
import { useCatalog, useDeletePreference, useSavePreference } from '@/features/traits/api';
import { errorMessage } from '@/lib/api/client';
import type { Preference } from '@/lib/api/types';
import { AppText } from '@/ui/AppText';
import { Avatar } from '@/ui/Avatar';
import { Button } from '@/ui/Button';
import { Chip } from '@/ui/Chip';
import { ErrorText } from '@/ui/ErrorText';
import { Icon } from '@/ui/Icon';
import { PhotoTile } from '@/ui/PhotoTile';
import { Sheet } from '@/ui/Sheet';
import { TextField } from '@/ui/TextField';
import { colors } from '@/ui/theme';

import { storeLabel } from './links';
import { SecretRow } from './SizeSheet';

type Props = { visible: boolean; subjectId: string; subjectName: string; isSelf?: boolean; entry?: Preference | null; onClose: () => void };

export function FavoriteSheet({ visible, subjectId, subjectName, isSelf, entry, onClose }: Props) {
  const catalog = useCatalog();
  const save = useSavePreference(subjectId);
  const remove = useDeletePreference(subjectId);
  const [category, setCategory] = useState('OTHER');
  const [brand, setBrand] = useState('');
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [links, setLinks] = useState<string[]>(['']);
  const [photo, setPhoto] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [secret, setSecret] = useState(false);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!visible) return;
    setCategory(entry?.category ?? 'OTHER');
    setBrand(entry?.brand ?? '');
    setName(entry?.name ?? '');
    setLinks(entry?.links.length ? entry.links.map((l) => l.url) : ['']);
    setPhoto(entry?.image?.url ?? null);
    setSecret(entry?.visibility === 'AUTHOR_ONLY');
    setError(null);
  }, [visible, entry]);

  async function pickPhoto() {
    const res = await ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync({ mediaTypes: ['images'], quality: 0.9 });
    if (!res.canceled && res.assets?.[0]) setPhoto(res.assets[0].uri);
  }

  async function submit() {
    if (!name.trim() && !brand.trim()) return setError('Nhập tên món hoặc hãng');
    const urls = links.map((l) => l.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    if (urls.some((u) => !/^https?:\/\/\S{3,}$/.test(u))) return setError('Link phải bắt đầu bằng http:// hoặc https://');
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const body: Record<string, unknown> = {
        category,
        brand: brand.trim() || undefined,
        name: name.trim() || undefined,
        visibility: secret ? 'AUTHOR_ONLY' : 'SHARED',
        links: urls.map((url) => ({ url, label: storeLabel(url) })),
      };
      if (!entry) body.subjectUserId = subjectId;
      if (photo && photo !== entry?.image?.url) body.imageMediaId = (await uploadImage(photo, { purpose: 'SHARED' })).id;
      Object.keys(body).forEach((k) => body[k] === undefined && delete body[k]);
      await save.mutateAsync({ id: entry?.id, body });
      onClose();
    } catch (e) {
      setError(errorMessage(e));
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  const categories = (catalog.data?.preferenceCategories ?? []).filter((c) => c.code !== 'HOBBY');
  return (
    <Sheet visible={visible} onClose={onClose} title={entry ? 'Sửa món thích' : isSelf ? 'Thêm món tôi thích' : `Thêm món ${subjectName} thích`}
      subtitle="Gợi nhớ khi cần chọn quà" right={<Avatar name={subjectName} tone={isSelf ? 'rose' : 'lavender'} size={32} />}>
      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 12 }}>
        <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Ảnh món" onPress={pickPhoto}>
          {photo ? <PhotoTile uri={photo} seed="fav" style={{ width: 88, height: 88 }} /> : (
            <View style={{ width: 88, height: 88, borderRadius: 20, borderWidth: 1.5, borderStyle: 'dashed', borderColor: colors.rose, backgroundColor: colors.roseSoft, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 4 }}>
              <Icon name="camera" size={22} color={colors.roseStrong} />
              <AppText variant="captionStrong" color={colors.roseStrong}>Ảnh món</AppText>
            </View>
          )}
        </Pressable>
        <View style={{ flex: 1, gap: 8 }}>
          <AppText variant="micro" color={colors.textMuted}>DANH MỤC</AppText>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 8 }}>
            {categories.map((c) => <Chip key={c.code} label={c.label} selected={category === c.code} onPress={() => setCategory(c.code)} />)}
          </View>
        </View>
      </View>
      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 10 }}>
        <View style={{ flex: 1 }}><TextField label="Hãng" value={brand} onChangeText={setBrand} placeholder="Keychron" /></View>
        <View style={{ flex: 1 }}><TextField label="Tên món" value={name} onChangeText={setName} placeholder="K2 Pro, switch nâu" /></View>
      </View>
      {links.map((l, i) => (
        <TextField key={i} label={`Link mua hàng ${i + 1}`} value={l} autoCapitalize="none" keyboardType="url" placeholder="https://shopee.vn/…"
          onChangeText={(t) => setLinks(links.map((x, j) => (j === i ? t : x)))} />
      ))}
      {links.length < 10 ? (
        <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" onPress={() => setLinks([...links, ''])}>
          <AppText variant="captionStrong" color={colors.roseStrong}>+ Thêm link khác</AppText>
        </Pressable>
      ) : null}
      {!isSelf ? <SecretRow value={secret} onChange={setSecret} name={subjectName} /> : null}
      <ErrorText message={error} />
      <Button title="Lưu món này" onPress={submit} loading={busy} />
      {entry ? <Button title="Xoá" variant="danger" onPress={async () => { await remove.mutateAsync(entry.id); onClose(); }} /> : null}
    </Sheet>
  );
}
```

```tsx
// src/features/partner/HobbySheet.tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

import { useSavePreference } from '@/features/traits/api';
import { errorMessage } from '@/lib/api/client';
import { Button } from '@/ui/Button';
import { ErrorText } from '@/ui/ErrorText';
import { Sheet } from '@/ui/Sheet';
import { TextField } from '@/ui/TextField';

export function HobbySheet({ visible, subjectId, onClose }: { visible: boolean; subjectId: string; onClose: () => void }) {
  const save = useSavePreference(subjectId);
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (visible) {
      setName('');
      setError(null);
    }
  }, [visible]);
  async function submit() {
    if (!name.trim()) return setError('Nhập sở thích');
    try {
      await save.mutateAsync({ body: { subjectUserId: subjectId, category: 'HOBBY', name: name.trim(), visibility: 'SHARED' } });
      onClose();
    } catch (e) {
      setError(errorMessage(e));
    }
  }
  return (
    <Sheet visible={visible} onClose={onClose} title="Thêm sở thích" subtitle="VD: Cà phê đen ít đường, ghét hành">
      <TextField label="Sở thích" value={name} onChangeText={setName} />
      <ErrorText message={error} />
      <Button title="Lưu" onPress={submit} loading={save.isPending} />
    </Sheet>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { Linking, Pressable, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';
import { useState } from 'react';

import { useMe } from '@/features/account/api';
import { ConnectCard } from '@/features/couple/ConnectCard';
import { useCouple } from '@/features/couple/api';
import { usePrediction } from '@/features/cycle/api';
import { daysUntilBirthday } from '@/features/home/milestone';
import { useCatalog, useTraits } from '@/features/traits/api';
import { canEdit, catalogLabel } from '@/features/traits/editing';
import type { Preference, SizeEntry } from '@/lib/api/types';
import { daysBetween, formatDate } from '@/lib/format';
import { AppText } from '@/ui/AppText';
import { Avatar } from '@/ui/Avatar';
import { Card } from '@/ui/Card';
import { Chip } from '@/ui/Chip';
import { Icon } from '@/ui/Icon';
import { IconCircle } from '@/ui/IconCircle';
import { PhotoTile } from '@/ui/PhotoTile';
import { Screen } from '@/ui/Screen';
import { colors } from '@/ui/theme';

import { FavoriteSheet } from './FavoriteSheet';
import { HobbySheet } from './HobbySheet';
import { storeLabel } from './links';
import { SizeSheet } from './SizeSheet';

const CARE_TIPS = ['Túi chườm ấm', 'Trà gừng', 'Socola'];

export default function PartnerScreen() {
  const me = useMe();
  const couple = useCouple();
  const partner = couple.data?.partner;
  const traits = useTraits(partner?.id);
  const catalog = useCatalog();
  const prediction = usePrediction(partner?.id, !!partner);
  const [sizeSheet, setSizeSheet] = useState<{ entry: SizeEntry | null } | null>(null);
  const [favSheet, setFavSheet] = useState<{ entry: Preference | null } | null>(null);
  const [hobbyOpen, setHobbyOpen] = useState(false);

  if (couple.isSuccess && !partner) {
    return <Screen><AppText variant="display">Người ấy</AppText><ConnectCard name={me.data?.displayName ?? 'B'} /></Screen>;
  }
  if (!partner) return <Screen><AppText color={colors.textMuted}>Đang tải…</AppText></Screen>;

  const sizes = traits.data?.sizes ?? [];
  const favorites = (traits.data?.preferences ?? []).filter((p) => p.category !== 'HOBBY');
  const hobbies = (traits.data?.preferences ?? []).filter((p) => p.category === 'HOBBY');
  const birthday = partner.birthday
    ? `Sinh nhật ${formatDate(partner.birthday).slice(0, 5)} · ${daysUntilBirthday(partner.birthday, new Date()) === 0 ? 'hôm nay' : `còn ${daysUntilBirthday(partner.birthday, new Date())} ngày`}`
    : 'Chưa có ngày sinh';
  const cycleDays = prediction.data ? -daysBetween(prediction.data.nextStart, new Date()) : null;
  const meId = me.data?.id ?? '';

  return (
    <Screen>
      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 14 }}>
        <Avatar name={partner.displayName} tone="lavender" size={56} uri={partner.avatarUrl} />
        <View style={{ gap: 4 }}>
          <AppText variant="display">{partner.displayName}</AppText>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
            <Icon name="gift" size={14} color={colors.peachInk} />
            <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted}>{birthday}</AppText>
          </View>
        </View>
      </View>

      {cycleDays !== null && cycleDays >= 0 ? (
        <View style={styles.cycle}>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12 }}>
            <IconCircle name="droplet" bg={colors.surface} color={colors.cycle} size={40} />
            <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              <AppText variant="bodyStrong">{cycleDays === 0 ? `Hôm nay có thể là ngày của ${partner.displayName}` : `Khoảng ${cycleDays} ngày nữa là tới ngày của ${partner.displayName}`}</AppText>
              <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted}>Dự kiến {formatDate(prediction.data!.nextStart).slice(0, 5)} · {partner.displayName} đang chia sẻ với bạn</AppText>
            </View>
          </View>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8 }}>
            {CARE_TIPS.map((t) => <View key={t} style={styles.tip}><AppText variant="captionStrong">{t}</AppText></View>)}
          </View>
        </View>
      ) : null}

      <Header title="Size" label="Thêm size" onAdd={() => setSizeSheet({ entry: null })} />
      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 10 }}>
        {sizes.length === 0 ? <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted}>Chưa có size nào.</AppText> : null}
        {sizes.map((s) => (
          <Pressable key={s.id} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel={`${catalogLabel(catalog.data?.sizeKinds ?? [], s.kind)} ${s.value}`}
            disabled={!canEdit(s, meId)} onPress={() => setSizeSheet({ entry: s })} style={{ width: '31%' }}>
            <Card style={{ padding: 12, gap: 2 }}>
              <AppText variant="micro" color={colors.textMuted}>{catalogLabel(catalog.data?.sizeKinds ?? [], s.kind).toUpperCase()}</AppText>
              <AppText variant="h2">{s.value}</AppText>
              {s.note ? <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted} numberOfLines={1}>{s.note}</AppText> : null}
              {s.visibility === 'AUTHOR_ONLY' ? <AppText variant="micro" color={colors.lavenderInk}>BÍ MẬT</AppText> : null}
            </Card>
          </Pressable>
        ))}
      </View>

      <Header title={`Món ${partner.displayName} thích`} label="Thêm món thích" onAdd={() => setFavSheet({ entry: null })} />
      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 12 }}>
        {favorites.length === 0 ? <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted}>Chưa có món nào.</AppText> : null}
        {favorites.map((p) => (
          <Pressable key={p.id} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel={p.name ?? p.brand ?? 'Món thích'} disabled={!canEdit(p, meId)}
            onPress={() => setFavSheet({ entry: p })} style={{ width: '47.5%' }}>
            <Card style={{ padding: 12, gap: 6 }}>
              <PhotoTile uri={p.image?.url} seed={p.id} style={{ height: 92, borderRadius: 16 }}>
                {p.visibility === 'AUTHOR_ONLY' ? (
                  <View style={styles.secret}><Icon name="lock" size={11} color={colors.white} /><AppText variant="micro" color={colors.white} style={{ letterSpacing: 0 }}>BÍ MẬT</AppText></View>
                ) : null}
              </PhotoTile>
              {p.brand ? <AppText variant="micro" color={colors.textMuted}>{p.brand.toUpperCase()}</AppText> : null}
              <AppText variant="bodyStrong" numberOfLines={2}>{p.name ?? catalogLabel(catalog.data?.preferenceCategories ?? [], p.category)}</AppText>
              {p.links[0] ? (
                <Pressable accessibilityRole="link" accessibilityLabel={p.links[0].label ?? storeLabel(p.links[0].url)} onPress={() => Linking.openURL(p.links[0].url)}
                  style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4 }}>
                  <Icon name="link" size={14} color={colors.textMuted} />
                  <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textMuted}>{p.links[0].label ?? storeLabel(p.links[0].url)}</AppText>
                </Pressable>
              ) : null}
            </Card>
          </Pressable>
        ))}
      </View>

      <AppText variant="h2">Sở thích khác</AppText>
      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 8 }}>
        {hobbies.map((h) => <Chip key={h.id} label={h.name ?? ''} selected={false} onPress={() => setFavSheet({ entry: h })} />)}
        <Chip label="+ Thêm" selected={false} onPress={() => setHobbyOpen(true)} />
      </View>

      <SizeSheet visible={!!sizeSheet} subjectId={partner.id} subjectName={partner.displayName} entry={sizeSheet?.entry} onClose={() => setSizeSheet(null)} />
      <FavoriteSheet visible={!!favSheet} subjectId={partner.id} subjectName={partner.displayName} entry={favSheet?.entry} onClose={() => setFavSheet(null)} />
      <HobbySheet visible={hobbyOpen} subjectId={partner.id} onClose={() => setHobbyOpen(false)} />
    </Screen>
  );
}

function Header({ title, label, onAdd }: { title: string; label: string; onAdd: () => void }) {
  return (
    <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
      <AppText variant="h2">{title}</AppText>
      <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel={label} onPress={onAdd} style={styles.add}>
        <Icon name="plus" size={14} color={colors.roseStrong} />
        <AppText variant="captionStrong" color={colors.roseStrong}>Thêm</AppText>
      </Pressable>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  cycle: { padding: 16, gap: 10, borderRadius: 24, backgroundColor: colors.roseSoft },
  tip: { height: 32, paddingHorizontal: 12, borderRadius: 999, backgroundColor: colors.surface, justifyContent: 'center' },
  add: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4, height: 32, paddingLeft: 10, paddingRight: 12, borderRadius: 999, backgroundColor: colors.roseSoft },
  secret: { position: 'absolute', left: 8, top: 8, flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4, paddingHorizontal: 8, paddingVertical: 4, borderRadius: 999, backgroundColor: colors.text },
});
```

```tsx
import { fireEvent, screen } from '@testing-library/react-native';
import { router } from 'expo-router';
import { Alert } from 'react-native';

import MeScreen from '@/features/me/MeScreen';
import { mockApi } from '@/test/api-mock';
import { renderWithProviders } from '@/test/render';

const ME = { id: 'u1', email: 'lan@gmail.com', displayName: 'Lan', handle: 'lan.2001', birthday: null, timezone: 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh', avatarUrl: null };
const SETTINGS = { shareLocation: true, shareCycle: true, cycleLengthDefault: 28, notifyPrefs: {}, discoverable: true, cycleTracking: false };
const COUPLE = { id: 'c1', startedDatingOn: '2025-02-14', createdAt: '', partner: { id: 'u2', displayName: 'Minh', handle: 'minh', birthday: null, avatarUrl: null } };

function routes(extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  return {
    'GET /api/me': { body: ME },
    'GET /api/me/settings': { body: SETTINGS },
    'GET /api/couples/me': { body: COUPLE },
    'PUT /api/me/settings': ({ body }: { body: object }) => ({ body: { ...SETTINGS, ...body } }),
    'POST /api/couples/me/end': { status: 204 },
    ...extra,
  };
}

describe('MeScreen', () => {
  test('shows profile, couple and opens sub screens', async () => {
    mockApi(routes());
    await renderWithProviders(<MeScreen />, { withSession: true });
    expect(await screen.findByText('Lan')).toBeOnTheScreen();
    expect(screen.getByText(/^Lan & Minh · \d+ ngày$/)).toBeOnTheScreen();
    expect(screen.getByText('Tuỳ chọn · đang tắt')).toBeOnTheScreen();
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Thông tin cá nhân' }));
    expect(router.push).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/me/profile');
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Theo dõi chu kỳ' }));
    expect(router.push).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/me/cycle');
  });

  test('privacy toggles update settings', async () => {
    const api = mockApi(routes());
    await renderWithProviders(<MeScreen />, { withSession: true });
    await fireEvent.press(await screen.findByRole('switch', { name: 'Chia sẻ vị trí' }));
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('switch', { name: 'Cho phép tìm theo ID' }));
    const bodies = api.calls.filter((c) => c.method === 'PUT').map((c) => c.body);
    expect(bodies).toEqual([{ shareLocation: false }, { discoverable: false }]);
  });

  test('ending the couple asks first and goes back to solo home', async () => {
    const alert = jest.spyOn(Alert, 'alert').mockImplementation((_t, _m, buttons) => buttons?.[1]?.onPress?.());
    const api = mockApi(routes());
    await renderWithProviders(<MeScreen />, { withSession: true });
    await fireEvent.press(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Kết thúc cặp đôi' }));
    expect(alert).toHaveBeenCalled();
    await screen.findByText('Lan');
    expect(api.calls.some((c) => c.path === '/api/couples/me/end')).toBe(true);
    expect(router.replace).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/');
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { fireEvent, screen } from '@testing-library/react-native';

import CycleScreen from '@/features/me/CycleScreen';
import { toIsoDate } from '@/lib/format';
import { mockApi } from '@/test/api-mock';
import { renderWithProviders } from '@/test/render';

const SETTINGS = { shareLocation: true, shareCycle: true, cycleLengthDefault: 28, notifyPrefs: {}, discoverable: true, cycleTracking: false };
const COUPLE = { id: 'c1', startedDatingOn: null, createdAt: '', partner: { id: 'u2', displayName: 'Minh', handle: 'minh', birthday: null, avatarUrl: null } };
const ME = { id: 'u1', email: '', displayName: 'Lan', handle: 'lan', birthday: null, timezone: 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh', avatarUrl: null };

describe('CycleScreen', () => {
  test('off by default; turning it on shows prediction and sharing', async () => {
    let settings = SETTINGS;
    const next = new Date();
    next.setDate(next.getDate() + 10);
    const api = mockApi({
      'GET /api/me': { body: ME },
      'GET /api/couples/me': { body: COUPLE },
      'GET /api/me/settings': () => ({ body: settings }),
      'PUT /api/me/settings': ({ body }: { body: object }) => { settings = { ...settings, ...body }; return { body: settings }; },
      'GET /api/cycles/logs': { body: [{ id: 'l1', periodStart: '2026-09-20', periodEnd: null }] },
      'GET /api/cycles/prediction': { body: { nextStart: toIsoDate(next), averageLength: 28, basedOnCycles: 2 } },
      'POST /api/cycles/logs': { status: 201, body: { id: 'l2', periodStart: toIsoDate(new Date()), periodEnd: null } },
    });
    await renderWithProviders(<CycleScreen />);
    expect(await screen.findByText(/Tính năng đang tắt/)).toBeOnTheScreen();

    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('switch', { name: 'Bật theo dõi chu kỳ' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('10')).toBeOnTheScreen();
    expect(screen.getByText('Minh thấy: “Khoảng 10 ngày nữa là tới ngày của Lan”')).toBeOnTheScreen();

    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Ghi nhận ngày bắt đầu' }));
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Xác nhận' }));
    await screen.findByText('10');
    expect(api.calls.find((c) => c.method === 'POST')?.body).toEqual({ periodStart: toIsoDate(new Date()) });
    expect(api.calls.filter((c) => c.method === 'PUT').map((c) => c.body)).toEqual([{ cycleTracking: true }]);
  });
});
```
